# C&C Generals Zero Hour Source Code: Headless Engine Analysis

## 1. Win32/DirectX Surface Area in GameEngine (NOT GameEngineDevice)

**Short answer: GameEngine/ is NOT clean of Win32 dependencies, but they are minimal and largely abstracted.**

### The Precompiled Header is the Biggest Problem

`/GameEngine/Include/Precompiled/PreRTS.h` (included as the mandatory first line of every `.cpp` in GameEngine) explicitly includes:

```cpp
#define WIN32_LEAN_AND_MEAN
#include <atlbase.h>
#include <windows.h>
#include <mmsystem.h>   // timeGetTime, timeBeginPeriod
#include <dinput.h>     // DirectInput 0x800
// also: imagehlp, shellapi, shlobj, wininet, winreg, vfw, lmcons, etc.
```

The comment in PreRTS.h is revealing: *"We actually don't use Windows for much other than timeGetTime, but it was included in 40 different .cpp files, so I bit the bullet and included it here."*

### Win32 usage inside GameEngine/Source/Common/GameEngine.cpp itself:

- `timeBeginPeriod(1)` / `timeEndPeriod(1)` in constructor/destructor (sets Windows timer resolution)
- `_Module.Init(NULL, ApplicationHInstance)` / `_Module.Term()` — ATL COM module, requires `HINSTANCE ApplicationHInstance` which is a Win32 global
- `extern HWND ApplicationHWnd` referenced in `execute()` loop (line 784)
- `timeGetTime()` used directly in the main loop for FPS limiting
- `extern CComModule _Module` — ATL dependency
- `extern bool DX8Wrapper_IsWindowed` — a global from the W3D renderer layer

### Other GameEngine files with Win32 includes:
- `GameEngine/Source/GameClient/MessageStream/LookAtXlat.cpp` — windows.h
- `GameEngine/Source/GameClient/GUI/IMEManager.cpp` — windows.h (Input Method Editor)
- `GameEngine/Include/GameClient/KeyDefs.h` — defines VK_ virtual key codes (requires windows.h context)
- `GameEngine/Include/GameNetwork/WOLBrowser/WebBrowser.h` — COM/browser

**Conclusion:** GameEngine/ itself has Win32 dependencies concentrated in:
1. The precompiled header (unavoidable as structured)
2. `GameEngine.cpp`: `timeBeginPeriod/End`, ATL `_Module`, `ApplicationHInstance` (HINSTANCE), `HWND ApplicationHWnd`, `timeGetTime()`
3. The GUI/keyboard subsystems inside GameClient sub-area

These are **not isolated** to GameEngineDevice. However, the *heavy* Win32+D3D+DirectInput+Miles+Bink dependencies are entirely in GameEngineDevice/.

---

## 2. GameEngine Initialization Chain

### Class Hierarchy
```
SubsystemInterface (abstract: init/reset/update)
  └── GameEngine (GameEngine/Include/Common/GameEngine.h)
        └── Win32GameEngine (GameEngineDevice/Include/Win32Device/Common/Win32GameEngine.h)
```

### GameEngine.h: The Abstract Interface
Key factory methods that **must** be overridden by a device implementation:
```cpp
virtual LocalFileSystem   *createLocalFileSystem()     = 0;
virtual ArchiveFileSystem *createArchiveFileSystem()   = 0;
virtual GameLogic         *createGameLogic()           = 0;
virtual GameClient        *createGameClient()          = 0;
virtual ModuleFactory     *createModuleFactory()       = 0;
virtual ThingFactory      *createThingFactory()        = 0;
virtual FunctionLexicon   *createFunctionLexicon()     = 0;
virtual Radar             *createRadar()               = 0;
virtual WebBrowser        *createWebBrowser()          = 0;
virtual ParticleSystemManager *createParticleSystemManager() = 0;
virtual AudioManager      *createAudioManager()        = 0;
```

Non-pure virtuals (have defaults):
```cpp
virtual FileSystem    *createFileSystem();     // non-pure, has default impl
virtual MessageStream *createMessageStream();  // non-pure, returns new MessageStream
virtual void serviceWindowsOS() {}             // empty default
virtual Bool isActive() { return m_isActive; }
```

### `GameEngine::init()` Initialization Order (GameEngine.cpp lines 251–704)

The subsystem initialization order is critical. Here is the complete ordered sequence:

1. `InitRandom()` — RNG seeding
2. `TheFileSystem = createFileSystem()` — base file system
3. `TheNameKeyGenerator` — string-to-int key mapping (never reset)
4. `TheCommandList` — command queue (never reset)
5. `XferCRC xferCRC` opened for INI CRC calculation
6. `TheLocalFileSystem` (via `createLocalFileSystem()`)
7. `TheArchiveFileSystem` (via `createArchiveFileSystem()`)
8. `TheWritableGlobalData` — loads `Data\INI\Default\GameData.ini` + `Data\INI\GameData.ini`
9. `parseCommandLine(argc, argv)`
10. `TheGameLODManager`
11. Water/Weather INI loads
12. `TheGameText` — string tables
13. `TheScienceStore`, `TheMultiplayerSettings`, `TheTerrainTypes`, `TheTerrainRoads`
14. `TheGlobalLanguageData`, `TheCDManager`
15. **`TheAudio = createAudioManager()`** — then immediately checks `isMusicAlreadyLoaded()` and **quits if false** (this is a critical blocker for headless mode)
16. `TheFunctionLexicon`, `TheModuleFactory`
17. `TheMessageStream` — the message bus
18. `TheSidesList`, `TheCaveSystem`, `TheRankInfoStore`, `ThePlayerTemplateStore`
19. `TheParticleSystemManager`
20. `TheFXListStore`, `TheWeaponStore`, `TheObjectCreationListStore`, `TheLocomotorStore`
21. `TheSpecialPowerStore`, `TheDamageFXStore`, `TheArmorStore`, `TheBuildAssistant`
22. `TheThingFactory` — loads `Data\INI\Default\Object.ini` + `Data\INI\Object\` directory
23. `TheUpgradeCenter`
24. **`TheGameClient = createGameClient()`**
25. `TheAI`
26. **`TheGameLogic = createGameLogic()`**
27. `TheTeamFactory`, `TheCrateSystem`, `ThePlayerList`
28. **`TheRecorder`** (replay system)
29. `TheRadar`, `TheVictoryConditions`
30. `TheMetaMap`, `TheActionManager`, `TheGameStateMap`, `TheGameState`
31. `TheGameResultsQueue`
32. `TheSubsystemList->postProcessLoadAll()` — cross-system post-init
33. `TheSubsystemList->resetAll()` — initial reset of all subsystems

**Critical blocker found:** After `TheAudio` is initialized, the code immediately calls:
```cpp
if (!TheAudio->isMusicAlreadyLoaded())
    setQuitting(TRUE);  // Engine quits before anything runs!
```
This means a null/stub audio manager must return `true` from `isMusicAlreadyLoaded()`.

### Entry Point Chain

```
WinMain()                     [Main/WinMain.cpp]
  → GameMain(argc, argv)      [GameEngine/Source/Common/GameMain.cpp]
      → TheGameEngine = CreateGameEngine()   [creates Win32GameEngine]
      → TheGameEngine->init(argc, argv)      [GameEngine.cpp, full subsystem init]
      → TheGameEngine->execute()             [the main loop, never returns until quit]
      → delete TheGameEngine
```

`CreateGameEngine()` is defined in `Main/WinMain.cpp` (not in a library) and simply returns `NEW Win32GameEngine`. For headless, you replace this with your own factory returning a `HeadlessGameEngine`.

### `GameEngine::update()` per-frame sequence:
```cpp
TheRadar->UPDATE();
TheAudio->UPDATE();
TheGameClient->UPDATE();          // renders frame, processes GUI
TheMessageStream->propagateMessages();
TheNetwork->UPDATE();             // if in network game
TheCDManager->UPDATE();
// then conditionally:
TheGameLogic->UPDATE();           // sim tick (skipped if paused or waiting for network)
```

### `GameEngine::execute()` main loop:
```cpp
while (!m_quitting) {
    update();
    // FPS limiter using timeGetTime() + Sleep(0)
    serviceWindowsOS();  // Win32 message pump (overridden in Win32GameEngine)
}
```

## 3. TheGameLogic — The Core Sim

`/GameEngine/Include/GameLogic/GameLogic.h`

### Class declaration:
```cpp
class GameLogic : public SubsystemInterface, public Snapshot
```

### Key interface methods:

**Subsystem lifecycle:**
```cpp
virtual void init();     // initializes terrain logic, object tracking, etc.
virtual void reset();    // clears all game data
virtual void update();   // one simulation tick
```

**Game start:**
```cpp
void prepareNewGame(Int gameMode, GameDifficulty diff, Int rankPoints);
void startNewGame(Bool loadSaveGame);   // "super hack" but it's the real entry point
void loadMapINI(AsciiString mapName);
```

**Game mode:**
```cpp
void setGameMode(Int mode);  // GAME_SINGLE_PLAYER, GAME_SKIRMISH, GAME_REPLAY, etc.
Int getGameMode();
Bool isInGame();
Bool isInSinglePlayerGame();
Bool isInSkirmishGame();
Bool isInReplayGame();
```

**Simulation frame query:**
```cpp
UnsignedInt getFrame();          // current frame number (0-based)
UnsignedInt getCRC(Int mode);    // get simulation CRC for sync verification
Bool isInGameLogicUpdate();      // true while update() is executing
```

**Object iteration:**
```cpp
Object *getFirstObject();        // get head of object linked list
// then: obj->getNextObject() to walk the list
Object *findObjectByID(ObjectID id);  // O(1) via vector index
void destroyObject(Object *obj);
```

**No "step N ticks" method exists.** The only tick mechanism is `update()` which advances by exactly one frame. To step N ticks, you call `update()` N times from outside. The `execute()` loop does exactly this. There is a game-pause mechanism:
```cpp
void setGamePaused(Bool paused, Bool pauseMusic = TRUE);
Bool isGamePaused();
```
When paused, `TheGameLogic->UPDATE()` is skipped in `GameEngine::update()`.

**Command processing:**
```cpp
void processCommandList(CommandList *list);  // called internally during update()
void logicMessageDispatcher(GameMessage *msg, void *userData);
```

**Victory:**
```cpp
void clearGameData(Bool showScoreScreen = TRUE);
```

## 5. Command/Action Injection

### Architecture: MessageStream → CommandList → GameLogic

The flow is:
1. Input events (keyboard/mouse/AI) are appended to `TheMessageStream`
2. `TheMessageStream->propagateMessages()` runs all attached `GameMessageTranslator`s in priority order
3. Translated commands fall through to `TheCommandList` (the final translator)
4. At the start of `TheGameLogic->update()`, `processCommandList(TheCommandList)` executes them

### GameMessage (`/GameEngine/Include/Common/MessageStream.h`)

```cpp
class GameMessage : public MemoryPoolObject {
    enum Type { /* ~200 message types */ };

    // append typed arguments
    void appendIntegerArgument(Int arg);
    void appendRealArgument(Real arg);
    void appendBooleanArgument(Bool arg);
    void appendObjectIDArgument(ObjectID arg);
    void appendLocationArgument(const Coord3D& arg);
    void appendPixelArgument(const ICoord2D& arg);
    void appendPixelRegionArgument(const IRegion2D& arg);

    Int getPlayerIndex() const;   // which player issued it
};
```

### How to inject a command from headless code:

Append to `TheMessageStream` (it will be translated/dispatched to `TheCommandList`):
```cpp
GameMessage *msg = TheMessageStream->appendMessage(GameMessage::MSG_DO_MOVETO);
msg->appendLocationArgument(targetPos);
// or for a new game:
GameMessage *msg = TheMessageStream->appendMessage(GameMessage::MSG_NEW_GAME);
msg->appendIntegerArgument(GAME_SINGLE_PLAYER);
msg->appendIntegerArgument(DIFFICULTY_NORMAL);
msg->appendIntegerArgument(0);  // rank points
```

Or directly to `TheCommandList` to bypass translators (for network messages / replays):
```cpp
GameMessage *msg = TheCommandList->appendMessage(GameMessage::MSG_DO_MOVETO);
```

### Key command message types for AI training:
```
MSG_NEW_GAME                   — start a game (with mode, difficulty, rank args)
MSG_DO_MOVETO                  — move selection to location
MSG_DO_ATTACKMOVETO            — attack-move to location
MSG_DO_ATTACK_OBJECT           — attack specific object
MSG_DO_FORCE_ATTACK_GROUND     — bombard location
MSG_DOZER_CONSTRUCT            — build a structure
MSG_QUEUE_UNIT_CREATE          — produce a unit
MSG_QUEUE_UPGRADE              — research an upgrade
MSG_SELL                       — sell a structure
MSG_AREA_SELECTION             — select units in rectangle (pixel coords)
MSG_CREATE_SELECTED_GROUP      — define selection from object IDs
MSG_SET_RALLY_POINT            — set rally point
MSG_DO_SPECIAL_POWER_AT_LOCATION / MSG_DO_SPECIAL_POWER_AT_OBJECT
MSG_PURCHASE_SCIENCE           — buy a general's power
```

Note: All network commands use integer IDs starting at `MSG_BEGIN_NETWORK_MESSAGES = 1000` with explicit comment: *"there should be NO IFDEFS OF ANY KIND from here until MSG_END_NETWORK_MESSAGES"* — these are the only messages that go over the wire, and they are the ones that get replayed.

## 6. Replay System

`/GameEngine/Include/Common/Recorder.h`

### RecorderClass interface:
```cpp
extern RecorderClass *TheRecorder;
RecorderClass *createRecorder();   // factory (device-implementation specific)

class RecorderClass : public SubsystemInterface {
    // Modes
    enum RecorderModeType {
        RECORDERMODETYPE_RECORD,
        RECORDERMODETYPE_PLAYBACK,
        RECORDERMODETYPE_NONE    // valid during shell / saved games
    };

    // Recording
    void updateRecord();               // called each frame during recording
    void stopRecording();

    // Playback
    Bool playbackFile(AsciiString filename);    // start replay playback
    Bool testVersionPlayback(AsciiString filename);
    void stopPlayback();
    void updatePlayback();             // called each frame during playback

    AsciiString getCurrentReplayFilename();
    RecorderModeType getMode();

    // File format
    struct ReplayHeader {
        AsciiString filename;
        Bool forPlayback;
        UnicodeString replayName;
        SYSTEMTIME timeVal;
        UnicodeString versionString, versionTimeString;
        UnsignedInt versionNumber;
        UnsignedInt exeCRC;          // executable CRC
        UnsignedInt iniCRC;          // INI data CRC (must match for valid playback)
        time_t startTime, endTime;
        UnsignedInt frameDuration;
        Bool quitEarly, desyncGame;
        Bool playerDiscons[MAX_SLOTS];
        AsciiString gameOptions;
        Int localPlayerIndex;
    };
    Bool readReplayHeader(ReplayHeader& header);
};
```

### How replays work:
- **Recording:** Every `GameMessage` between `MSG_BEGIN_NETWORK_MESSAGES` (1000) and `MSG_END_NETWORK_MESSAGES` (1999) that flows through `TheCommandList` is written to a `.rep` file along with its frame number. `TheRecorder->writeToFile(msg)` is called in the command dispatch path.
- **Playback:** `TheRecorder->updatePlayback()` reads the next frame number and appends the stored `GameMessage`s directly to `TheCommandList` at the correct frame, bypassing `TheMessageStream`. This means replays are **purely deterministic command re-injection** — they don't record visual state.
- **CRC verification:** Every frame during network/replay, `MSG_LOGIC_CRC` messages exchange CRCs computed by `TheGameLogic->getCRC()` to detect desync.
- **File extension:** `.rep` files (confirmed in `GameEngine.cpp` line 650: `fname.endsWithNoCase(".rep")`)

**For AI training, the replay system is extremely valuable:** You can record a game, then play it back programmatically by calling `TheRecorder->playbackFile()` and then driving `TheGameEngine->update()` in a loop, harvesting state each tick.

## 7. GameEngineDevice Interface

### Win32GameEngine overrides GameEngine's pure virtuals:

```cpp
// Win32GameEngine.h — what Win32 implements
class Win32GameEngine : public GameEngine {
    GameLogic         *createGameLogic()          { return NEW W3DGameLogic; }
    GameClient        *createGameClient()          { return NEW W3DGameClient; }
    ModuleFactory     *createModuleFactory()       { return NEW W3DModuleFactory; }
    ThingFactory      *createThingFactory()        { return NEW W3DThingFactory; }
    FunctionLexicon   *createFunctionLexicon()     { return NEW W3DFunctionLexicon; }
    LocalFileSystem   *createLocalFileSystem()     { return NEW Win32LocalFileSystem; }
    ArchiveFileSystem *createArchiveFileSystem()   { return NEW Win32BIGFileSystem; }
    ParticleSystemManager *createParticleSystemManager() { return NEW W3DParticleSystemManager; }
    NetworkInterface  *createNetwork()             { return NetworkInterface::createNetwork(); }
    Radar             *createRadar()               { return NEW W3DRadar; }
    WebBrowser        *createWebBrowser()          { return NEW CComObject<W3DWebBrowser>; }
    AudioManager      *createAudioManager()        { return NEW MilesAudioManager; }
};
```

### Device dependencies by subsystem:

| Subsystem | Win32 Impl | External SDK |
|-----------|-----------|--------------|
| GameLogic | `W3DGameLogic` | None (thin wrapper: only adds `W3DTerrainLogic`, `W3DGhostObjectManager`) |
| GameClient | `W3DGameClient` | W3D renderer, DirectInput, Bink video |
| ModuleFactory | `W3DModuleFactory` | W3D renderable modules |
| ThingFactory | `W3DThingFactory` | W3D assets |
| FunctionLexicon | `W3DFunctionLexicon` | None |
| LocalFileSystem | `Win32LocalFileSystem` | Win32 API |
| ArchiveFileSystem | `Win32BIGFileSystem` | Win32 API |
| AudioManager | `MilesAudioManager` | **Miles Sound System (MSS), DirectSound** |
| Radar | `W3DRadar` | W3D renderer |
| WebBrowser | `W3DWebBrowser` | IE COM/D3D8 |
| ParticleSystemManager | `W3DParticleSystemManager` | W3D renderer |

### GameClient is the heaviest dependency:

`W3DGameClient` includes (from `W3DGameClient.h`):
- `W3DDisplay.h` → D3D8 rendering
- `W3DInGameUI.h` → UI rendering
- `W3DTerrainVisual.h` → terrain rendering
- `W3DGameWindowManager.h` → window system
- `BinkVideoPlayer.h` → Bink video
- `Win32DIKeyboard.h` → DirectInput 8
- `Win32DIMouse.h` → DirectInput 8
- `Win32Mouse.h` → Win32 mouse
- `W3DMouse.h` → W3D cursor rendering

## 8. Entry Point and Minimal Initialization for Headless

### There is no existing headless/server/test mode

No `#ifdef HEADLESS`, no console entry point, no existing server mode. The entire codebase assumes `WinMain`. However, the architecture is cleanly factored at the `CreateGameEngine()` level.

### Minimal headless initialization sequence

The key insight is that `GameMain()` is the real entry point — it just needs `CreateGameEngine()` to return a headless engine instead of `Win32GameEngine`. Here is the complete plan:

**Step 1: Create `HeadlessGameEngine : public GameEngine`**

Override all pure virtual factory methods:
```cpp
class HeadlessGameEngine : public GameEngine {
    // File system — these can stay Win32 or be cross-platform
    LocalFileSystem   *createLocalFileSystem()  { return NEW Win32LocalFileSystem; }
    ArchiveFileSystem *createArchiveFileSystem() { return NEW Win32BIGFileSystem; }

    // Logic — W3DGameLogic only adds terrain+ghost object managers
    // You either use W3DGameLogic (requires W3D) or create HeadlessGameLogic
    GameLogic         *createGameLogic()         { return NEW HeadlessGameLogic; }

    // Client — STUB: all methods are no-ops
    GameClient        *createGameClient()        { return NEW HeadlessGameClient; }

    // Module/Thing factories — need W3D or significant stub work
    ModuleFactory     *createModuleFactory()     { return NEW HeadlessModuleFactory; }
    ThingFactory      *createThingFactory()      { return NEW HeadlessThingFactory; }
    FunctionLexicon   *createFunctionLexicon()   { return NEW HeadlessFunctionLexicon; }

    // Audio — CRITICAL: isMusicAlreadyLoaded() MUST return true
    AudioManager      *createAudioManager()      { return NEW NullAudioManager; }

    // Radar — stub
    Radar             *createRadar()             { return NEW NullRadar; }

    // WebBrowser — stub (nullptr may crash; needs a null object)
    WebBrowser        *createWebBrowser()        { return NEW NullWebBrowser; }

    // Particles — stub
    ParticleSystemManager *createParticleSystemManager() { return NEW NullParticleSystemManager; }

    // Override serviceWindowsOS to do nothing (no message pump needed)
    void serviceWindowsOS() {}
};
```

**Step 2: The HWND / HINSTANCE problem**

The base `GameEngine.cpp` constructor calls:
```cpp
_Module.Init(NULL, ApplicationHInstance);  // ATL COM init
```
And `ApplicationHInstance` is a Win32 global set in `WinMain`. For headless, you either:
- Initialize it to a null/dummy value, or
- Call `GetModuleHandle(NULL)` to get a valid HINSTANCE without a window

The `execute()` loop also references `extern HWND ApplicationHWnd` but only passes it to D3D reset functions that you won't be calling. Setting it to NULL is safe for headless.

**Step 3: The AudioManager "music loaded" gate**

In `GameEngine::init()`:
```cpp
initSubsystem(TheAudio, "TheAudio", createAudioManager(), NULL);
if (!TheAudio->isMusicAlreadyLoaded())
    setQuitting(TRUE);  // Engine halts here!
```

Your `NullAudioManager` must override `isMusicAlreadyLoaded()` to return `true`. The base class `AudioManager::isMusicAlreadyLoaded()` is declared at line 292 of GameAudio.h.

**Step 4: Replace WinMain with a plain `main()`**

```cpp
// headless_main.cpp
#include <windows.h>  // still needed for HINSTANCE and ATL
#include "Common/GameEngine.h"

extern HINSTANCE ApplicationHInstance;
extern HWND ApplicationHWnd;

GameEngine *CreateGameEngine() {
    return new HeadlessGameEngine();
}

int main(int argc, char *argv[]) {
    ApplicationHInstance = GetModuleHandle(NULL);
    ApplicationHWnd = NULL;
    GameMain(argc, argv);
    return 0;
}
```

**Step 5: Custom execute() loop for controlled stepping**

Override `execute()` to allow external control rather than the internal `while (!m_quitting)` spin:

```cpp
class HeadlessGameEngine : public GameEngine {
public:
    void execute() override {
        // Don't run the loop internally — let the caller drive it
        // Caller calls stepFrame() repeatedly instead
    }

    Bool stepFrame() {
        if (m_quitting) return false;
        update();       // one tick of GameLogic + all subsystems
        return true;
    }
};
```

Then your AI training harness:
```cpp
engine->init(argc, argv);

// Start a game
GameMessage *msg = TheMessageStream->appendMessage(GameMessage::MSG_NEW_GAME);
msg->appendIntegerArgument(GAME_SKIRMISH);
msg->appendIntegerArgument(DIFFICULTY_NORMAL);
msg->appendIntegerArgument(0);

// Run simulation
for (int frame = 0; frame < 10000 && engine->stepFrame(); frame++) {
    // Observe state
    UnsignedInt f = TheGameLogic->getFrame();
    for (Object *obj = TheGameLogic->getFirstObject(); obj; obj = obj->getNextObject()) {
        Player *owner = obj->getControllingPlayer();
        const Coord3D *pos = obj->getPosition();
        // emit training observation...
    }
    // Inject AI actions
    GameMessage *move = TheCommandList->appendMessage(GameMessage::MSG_DO_MOVETO);
    move->appendLocationArgument(targetPos);
}
```

---

## Key Findings Summary

| Question | Finding |
|----------|---------|
| Win32 in GameEngine? | Yes — PreRTS.h includes windows.h + dinput.h for all TUs; GameEngine.cpp uses `timeGetTime`, ATL `_Module`, `HINSTANCE`, `HWND` |
| Win32 isolated to GameEngineDevice? | No, but the heavy D3D/DInput/Miles/Bink is entirely in GameEngineDevice |
| Single frame step? | No built-in method; call `GameEngine::update()` once per tick; `execute()` is just a `while(!m_quitting)` loop calling `update()` |
| Object iteration | `TheGameLogic->getFirstObject()` + `obj->getNextObject()` linked list; O(1) lookup via `findObjectByID()` |
| Player iteration | `ThePlayerList->getPlayerCount()` + `getNthPlayer(i)` |
| Command injection | `TheMessageStream->appendMessage(type)` + append typed args; or directly to `TheCommandList` |
| Replay mechanism | `MSG_BEGIN_NETWORK_MESSAGES`..`MSG_END_NETWORK_MESSAGES` recorded to `.rep` file by frame; played back by re-injecting into `TheCommandList` |
| Critical headless blocker | `TheAudio->isMusicAlreadyLoaded()` must return `true` or engine immediately sets `m_quitting = TRUE` |
| Entry point | `GameMain()` in `GameEngine/Source/Common/GameMain.cpp` calls `CreateGameEngine()` then `init()` then `execute()` |
| Module/ThingFactory | Cannot be easily stubbed — these factories create all unit behavior modules; W3D-specific factories assume W3D asset loading. This is the deepest coupling point |
```

Excellent analysis. Now I have everything to write an accurate Plan 1. Key findings that shape the plan:

- Engine is deeply Win32-tied — must build on Windows (Intel Mac VM)
- `NullAudioManager::isMusicAlreadyLoaded()` must return `true` (critical blocker)
- Frame stepping = call `update()` in a loop, override `execute()` to allow external control
- Command injection via `TheMessageStream->appendMessage()` 
- State extraction via `ThePlayerList` + `TheGameLogic->getFirstObject()` linked list
- ModuleFactory/ThingFactory are the deepest coupling — need careful stubbing

1024;\nstatic const int SPATIAL_RES = 128;\nstatic const int SPATIAL_CHANNELS = 40;\nstatic const int ENTITY_FEATURE_DIM = 32;\n\n// --- Entity features (index definitions) ---\n// These indices define what each feature slot in the entity vector means\nenum EntityFeatureIndex {\n    FEAT_UNIT_TYPE       = 0,   // integer type ID\n    FEAT_FACTION         = 1,   // 0=USA, 1=China, 2=GLA\n    FEAT_PLAYER_IDX      = 2,   // 0-7\n    FEAT_ALLIANCE        = 3,   // 0=enemy, 1=neutral, 2=allied\n    FEAT_HEALTH_PCT      = 4,   // 0.0 - 1.0\n    FEAT_X               = 5,   // normalized world x [0, 1]\n    FEAT_Y               = 6,   // normalized world y [0, 1]\n    FEAT_CURRENT_ACTION  = 7,   // action type enum\n    FEAT_VETERANCY       = 8,   // 0=regular, 1=veteran, 2=elite, 3=heroic\n    FEAT_IS_GARRISONED   = 9,   // bool\n    FEAT_SQUAD_SIZE      = 10,  // 1-5 for infantry squads\n    FEAT_IS_BUILDING     = 11,  // bool\n    FEAT_IS_AIR          = 12,  // bool\n    FEAT_SPEED           = 13,  // movement speed (normalized)\n    FEAT_DPS             = 14,  // damage per second (normalized)\n    // 15-31: reserved for future features\n    ENTITY_FEATURE_COUNT = 32\n};\n\n// --- Observation struct ---\nstruct EntityObservation {\n    float features[ENTITY_FEATURE_COUNT];\n    bool valid;  // true if this slot is occupied\n};\n\nstruct PlayerObservation {\n    float money;\n    float power_surplus;\n    float supply_used;\n    float supply_cap;\n    int   num_units;\n    int   tech_level;\n    int   faction_id;   // 0=USA, 1=China, 2=GLA, -1=empty slot\n    int   team_id;\n    bool  is_alive;\n};\n\nstruct GameStateObservation {\n    uint32_t frame;\n    float game_time_seconds;\n    bool  game_over;\n    int   winner_player_idx;  // -1 if not over\n\n    PlayerObservation players[MAX_PLAYERS];\n    EntityObservation entities[MAX_ENTITIES];\n    int               entity_count;\n\n    // Spatial map: [SPATIAL_CHANNELS][SPATIAL_RES][SPATIAL_RES]\n    // Laid out flat for easy memcpy\n    float spatial[SPATIAL_CHANNELS * SPATIAL_RES * SPATIAL_RES];\n};\n\n// --- Action struct ---\nenum ActionType {\n    ACT_BUILD_STRUCTURE      = 0,\n    ACT_TRAIN_UNIT           = 1,\n    ACT_SELL_BUILDING        = 2,\n    ACT_SET_RALLY_POINT      = 3,\n    ACT_MOVE                 = 4,\n    ACT_ATTACK_MOVE          = 5,\n    ACT_PATROL               = 6,\n    ACT_GUARD                = 7,\n    ACT_ENTER_GARRISON       = 8,\n    ACT_ATTACK_TARGET        = 9,\n    ACT_FORCE_FIRE           = 10,\n    ACT_RETREAT              = 11,\n    ACT_USE_ABILITY          = 12,\n    ACT_RESEARCH_UPGRADE     = 13,\n    ACT_CHOOSE_GENERAL_POWER = 14,\n    ACT_ACTIVATE_POWER       = 15,\n    ACT_SELECT_GROUP         = 16,\n    ACT_SET_WAYPOINT         = 17,\n    ACT_GARRISON_BUILDING    = 18,\n    ACT_EXIT_GARRISON        = 19,\n    ACT_NOOP                 = 20,\n    ACTION_TYPE_COUNT        = 21\n};\n\nstruct ActionCommand {\n    int      action_type;         // ActionType enum\n    int      target_entity_idx;   // -1 if no target entity\n    float    target_x;            // world coordinates, -1 if no position target\n    float    target_y;\n    int      modifier;            // action-specific (unit type ID, upgrade ID, etc.)\n    int      player_idx;          // which player is acting (0-based)\n};\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Create StateExtractor.h**\n\n```cpp\n// src/engine/state/StateExtractor.h\n#pragma once\n\n#include \"Protocol.h\"\n\n/**\n * StateExtractor — reads current game state from the running engine.\n *\n * Walks ThePlayerList and TheGameLogic object list to fill\n * a GameStateObservation struct. Called after each stepFrame().\n */\nclass StateExtractor {\npublic:\n    StateExtractor();\n\n    // Fill obs with current game state. Call after stepFrame().\n    void extract(GameStateObservation& obs) const;\n\n    // Set map dimensions for coordinate normalization\n    void setMapDimensions(float width, float height);\n\nprivate:\n    float m_mapWidth  = 1.0f;\n    float m_mapHeight = 1.0f;\n\n    void extractPlayers(GameStateObservation& obs) const;\n    void extractEntities(GameStateObservation& obs) const;\n    void extractSpatialMap(GameStateObservation& obs) const;\n\n    // Convert world coords to [0, 1] normalized range\n    float normalizeX(float worldX) const { return worldX / m_mapWidth; }\n    float normalizeY(float worldY) const { return worldY / m_mapHeight; }\n\n    // Map entity to faction index (0=USA, 1=China, 2=GLA, -1=unknown)\n    int getFactionIndex(const class Player* player) const;\n\n    // Fill entity feature vector from a game Object\n    void fillEntityFeatures(const class Object* obj, float* features) const;\n};\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: Create StateExtractor.cpp**\n\n```cpp\n// src/engine/state/StateExtractor.cpp\n#include \"PreRTS.h\"\n#include \"StateExtractor.h\"\n\n// Engine globals and types\n#include \"GameLogic/GameLogic.h\"\n#include \"Common/PlayerList.h\"\n#include \"Common/Player.h\"\n#include \"GameLogic/Object.h\"\n#include \"Common/ThingTemplate.h\"\n#include \"GameLogic/Module/BodyModule.h\"\n\nStateExtractor::StateExtractor() {}\n\nvoid StateExtractor::setMapDimensions(float width, float height) {\n    m_mapWidth  = (width  > 0.0f) ? width  : 1.0f;\n    m_mapHeight = (height > 0.0f) ? height : 1.0f;\n}\n\nvoid StateExtractor::extract(GameStateObservation& obs) const {\n    // Zero out the entire observation\n    memset(&obs, 0, sizeof(obs));\n\n    obs.frame            = TheGameLogic->getFrame();\n    obs.game_time_seconds = (float)obs.frame / 30.0f;  // 30 fps\n    obs.game_over        = !TheGameLogic->isInGame();\n    obs.winner_player_idx = -1;  // TODO: detect victory condition\n\n    extractPlayers(obs);\n    extractEntities(obs);\n    extractSpatialMap(obs);\n}\n\nvoid StateExtractor::extractPlayers(GameStateObservation& obs) const {\n    if (!ThePlayerList) return;\n\n    int count = ThePlayerList->getPlayerCount();\n    for (int i = 0; i < count && i < MAX_PLAYERS; i++) {\n        Player* p = ThePlayerList->getNthPlayer(i);\n        if (!p) continue;\n\n        PlayerObservation& po = obs.players[i];\n        po.faction_id = getFactionIndex(p);\n        po.team_id    = (int)p->getPlayerIndex();\n        po.is_alive   = TRUE;  // TODO: check defeat condition\n\n        // Money\n        if (Money* money = p->getMoney()) {\n            po.money = (float)money->countMoney();\n        }\n\n        // Count units owned by this player\n        int unitCount = 0;\n        for (Object* obj = TheGameLogic->getFirstObject(); obj; obj = obj->getNextObject()) {\n            if (obj->getControllingPlayer() == p && !obj->isDestroyed()) {\n                unitCount++;\n            }\n        }\n        po.num_units = unitCount;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid StateExtractor::extractEntities(GameStateObservation& obs) const {\n    if (!TheGameLogic) return;\n\n    int idx = 0;\n    for (Object* obj = TheGameLogic->getFirstObject();\n         obj && idx < MAX_ENTITIES;\n         obj = obj->getNextObject()) {\n\n        if (obj->isDestroyed()) continue;\n\n        EntityObservation& eo = obs.entities[idx];\n        eo.valid = true;\n        fillEntityFeatures(obj, eo.features);\n        idx++;\n    }\n    obs.entity_count = idx;\n}\n\nvoid StateExtractor::fillEntityFeatures(const Object* obj, float* f) const {\n    const Coord3D* pos = obj->getPosition();\n    Player* owner = obj->getControllingPlayer();\n\n    f[FEAT_PLAYER_IDX]   = owner ? (float)owner->getPlayerIndex() : 0.0f;\n    f[FEAT_FACTION]      = (float)getFactionIndex(owner);\n    f[FEAT_X]            = pos ? normalizeX(pos->x) : 0.0f;\n    f[FEAT_Y]            = pos ? normalizeY(pos->y) : 0.0f;\n    f[FEAT_VETERANCY]    = (float)obj->getVeterancyLevel();\n    f[FEAT_IS_BUILDING]  = obj->isKindOf(KINDOF_STRUCTURE) ? 1.0f : 0.0f;\n\n    // Health percentage\n    if (BodyModuleInterface* body = obj->getBodyModule()) {\n        float maxHp = (float)body->getMaxHealth();\n        float curHp = (float)body->getHealth();\n        f[FEAT_HEALTH_PCT] = (maxHp > 0.0f) ? (curHp / maxHp) : 0.0f;\n    }\n\n    // Unit type ID from template name hash\n    if (const ThingTemplate* tmpl = obj->getTemplate()) {\n        f[FEAT_UNIT_TYPE] = (float)(tmpl->getNameKey() & 0xFFFF);\n    }\n}\n\nvoid StateExtractor::extractSpatialMap(GameStateObservation& obs) const {\n    // Spatial map layout: [channel][row][col]\n    // Channel layout per player slot (3 channels each):\n    //   player_i * 3 + 0 = unit density\n    //   player_i * 3 + 1 = unit DPS sum\n    //   player_i * 3 + 2 = unit HP sum\n    // Channels MAX_PLAYERS*3 onwards: buildings, terrain, fog (future)\n\n    auto cellIdx = [](int ch, int row, int col) -> int {\n        return ch * SPATIAL_RES * SPATIAL_RES + row * SPATIAL_RES + col;\n    };\n\n    if (!TheGameLogic) return;\n\n    for (Object* obj = TheGameLogic->getFirstObject(); obj; obj = obj->getNextObject()) {\n        if (obj->isDestroyed()) continue;\n\n        const Coord3D* pos = obj->getPosition();\n        if (!pos) continue;\n\n        Player* owner = obj->getControllingPlayer();\n        if (!owner) continue;\n\n        int pIdx = (int)owner->getPlayerIndex();\n        if (pIdx >= MAX_PLAYERS) continue;\n\n        // Grid cell\n        int col = (int)(normalizeX(pos->x) * (SPATIAL_RES - 1));\n        int row = (int)(normalizeY(pos->y) * (SPATIAL_RES - 1));\n        col = max(0, min(SPATIAL_RES - 1, col));\n        row = max(0, min(SPATIAL_RES - 1, row));\n\n        int densityCh = pIdx * 3 + 0;\n        obs.spatial[cellIdx(densityCh, row, col)] += 1.0f;  // count\n        // DPS and HP channels: TODO when weapon/health data is available\n    }\n}\n\nint StateExtractor::getFactionIndex(const Player* p) const {\n    if (!p) return -1;\n    AsciiString side = p->getBaseSide();\n    if (side == \"America\") return 0;\n    if (side == \"China\")   return 1;\n    if (side == \"GLA\")     return 2;\n    return -1;\n}\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: Commit**\n\n```bash\ngit add src/engine/state/\ngit commit -m \"feat(engine): state extraction layer — entities, players, spatial map\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### Task 9: Action Injection Layer\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `src/engine/state/ActionInjector.h`\n- Create: `src/engine/state/ActionInjector.cpp`\n\nTranslates `ActionCommand` structs into `GameMessage`s injected into `TheMessageStream`.\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Create ActionInjector.h**\n\n```cpp\n// src/engine/state/ActionInjector.h\n#pragma once\n\n#include \"Protocol.h\"\n\n/**\n * ActionInjector — translates ActionCommand structs into GameMessages.\n *\n * Injects commands via TheMessageStream (normal path)\n * or TheCommandList (direct, bypasses translators).\n *\n * All coordinates in ActionCommand are in world space.\n */\nclass ActionInjector {\npublic:\n    ActionInjector() = default;\n\n    // Inject an action for the given player. Returns true on success.\n    Bool inject(const ActionCommand& cmd) const;\n\nprivate:\n    Bool injectMove(const ActionCommand& cmd) const;\n    Bool injectAttackMove(const ActionCommand& cmd) const;\n    Bool injectAttackTarget(const ActionCommand& cmd) const;\n    Bool injectBuildStructure(const ActionCommand& cmd) const;\n    Bool injectTrainUnit(const ActionCommand& cmd) const;\n    Bool injectSell(const ActionCommand& cmd) const;\n    Bool injectUseAbility(const ActionCommand& cmd) const;\n    Bool injectResearchUpgrade(const ActionCommand& cmd) const;\n    Bool injectNoop(const ActionCommand& cmd) const;\n};\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Create ActionInjector.cpp**\n\n```cpp\n// src/engine/state/ActionInjector.cpp\n#include \"PreRTS.h\"\n#include \"ActionInjector.h\"\n\n#include \"Common/MessageStream.h\"\n#include \"GameLogic/GameLogic.h\"\n\nBool ActionInjector::inject(const ActionCommand& cmd) const {\n    switch (cmd.action_type) {\n        case ACT_MOVE:            return injectMove(cmd);\n        case ACT_ATTACK_MOVE:     return injectAttackMove(cmd);\n        case ACT_ATTACK_TARGET:   return injectAttackTarget(cmd);\n        case ACT_BUILD_STRUCTURE: return injectBuildStructure(cmd);\n        case ACT_TRAIN_UNIT:      return injectTrainUnit(cmd);\n        case ACT_SELL_BUILDING:   return injectSell(cmd);\n        case ACT_USE_ABILITY:     return injectUseAbility(cmd);\n        case ACT_RESEARCH_UPGRADE:return injectResearchUpgrade(cmd);\n        case ACT_NOOP:            return injectNoop(cmd);\n        default:\n            return FALSE;\n    }\n}\n\nBool ActionInjector::injectMove(const ActionCommand& cmd) const {\n    if (!TheMessageStream) return FALSE;\n    Coord3D pos = { cmd.target_x, cmd.target_y, 0.0f };\n    GameMessage* msg = TheMessageStream->appendMessage(GameMessage::MSG_DO_MOVETO);\n    if (!msg) return FALSE;\n    msg->appendLocationArgument(pos);\n    return TRUE;\n}\n\nBool ActionInjector::injectAttackMove(const ActionCommand& cmd) const {\n    if (!TheMessageStream) return FALSE;\n    Coord3D pos = { cmd.target_x, cmd.target_y, 0.0f };\n    GameMessage* msg = TheMessageStream->appendMessage(GameMessage::MSG_DO_ATTACKMOVETO);\n    if (!msg) return FALSE;\n    msg->appendLocationArgument(pos);\n    return TRUE;\n}\n\nBool ActionInjector::injectAttackTarget(const ActionCommand& cmd) const {\n    if (!TheMessageStream || cmd.target_entity_idx < 0) return FALSE;\n    Object* target = TheGameLogic->findObjectByID((ObjectID)cmd.target_entity_idx);\n    if (!target) return FALSE;\n    GameMessage* msg = TheMessageStream->appendMessage(GameMessage::MSG_DO_ATTACK_OBJECT);\n    if (!msg) return FALSE;\n    msg->appendObjectIDArgument(target->getID());\n    return TRUE;\n}\n\nBool ActionInjector::injectBuildStructure(const ActionCommand& cmd) const {\n    // cmd.modifier = ThingTemplate name key for the structure to build\n    // cmd.target_x, target_y = placement position\n    if (!TheMessageStream) return FALSE;\n    Coord3D pos = { cmd.target_x, cmd.target_y, 0.0f };\n    GameMessage* msg = TheMessageStream->appendMessage(GameMessage::MSG_DOZER_CONSTRUCT);\n    if (!msg) return FALSE;\n    msg->appendIntegerArgument(cmd.modifier);  // structure type key\n    msg->appendLocationArgument(pos);\n    return TRUE;\n}\n\nBool ActionInjector::injectTrainUnit(const ActionCommand& cmd) const {\n    // cmd.modifier = ThingTemplate name key for unit to train\n    // cmd.target_entity_idx = producing building ObjectID\n    if (!TheMessageStream) return FALSE;\n    GameMessage* msg = TheMessageStream->appendMessage(GameMessage::MSG_QUEUE_UNIT_CREATE);\n    if (!msg) return FALSE;\n    msg->appendIntegerArgument(cmd.modifier);  // unit type key\n    return TRUE;\n}\n\nBool ActionInjector::injectSell(const ActionCommand& cmd) const {\n    if (!TheMessageStream || cmd.target_entity_idx < 0) return FALSE;\n    Object* building = TheGameLogic->findObjectByID((ObjectID)cmd.target_entity_idx);\n    if (!building) return FALSE;\n    GameMessage* msg = TheMessageStream->appendMessage(GameMessage::MSG_SELL);\n    if (!msg) return FALSE;\n    msg->appendObjectIDArgument(building->getID());\n    return TRUE;\n}\n\nBool ActionInjector::injectUseAbility(const ActionCommand& cmd) const {\n    if (!TheMessageStream) return FALSE;\n    Coord3D pos = { cmd.target_x, cmd.target_y, 0.0f };\n    GameMessage* msg = TheMessageStream->appendMessage(\n        GameMessage::MSG_DO_SPECIAL_POWER_AT_LOCATION);\n    if (!msg) return FALSE;\n    msg->appendIntegerArgument(cmd.modifier);  // special power key\n    msg->appendLocationArgument(pos);\n    return TRUE;\n}\n\nBool ActionInjector::injectResearchUpgrade(const ActionCommand& cmd) const {\n    if (!TheMessageStream) return FALSE;\n    GameMessage* msg = TheMessageStream->appendMessage(GameMessage::MSG_QUEUE_UPGRADE);\n    if (!msg) return FALSE;\n    msg->appendIntegerArgument(cmd.modifier);  // upgrade type key\n    return TRUE;\n}\n\nBool ActionInjector::injectNoop(const ActionCommand& cmd) const {\n    return TRUE;  // no-op: do nothing\n}\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: Commit**\n\n```bash\ngit add src/engine/state/ActionInjector.h src/engine/state/ActionInjector.cpp\ngit commit -m \"feat(engine): action injection layer — all action types → GameMessage\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### Task 10: ZeroMQ Training Server\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `src/engine/server/TrainingServer.h`\n- Create: `src/engine/server/TrainingServer.cpp`\n\nPython training loop connects here to step frames, read state, and send actions.\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Add ZeroMQ to CMakeLists.txt**\n\nAppend to `src/engine/CMakeLists.txt`:\n```cmake\n# ZeroMQ for Python-C++ communication\nfind_package(ZeroMQ REQUIRED)\ntarget_link_libraries(zh_headless PRIVATE libzmq)\ntarget_include_directories(zh_headless PRIVATE ${ZeroMQ_INCLUDE_DIRS})\n```\n\nInstall ZeroMQ in Windows VM:\n```\nvcpkg install zeromq:x86-windows\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Create TrainingServer.h**\n\n```cpp\n// src/engine/server/TrainingServer.h\n#pragma once\n\n#include \"../state/Protocol.h\"\n#include \"../state/StateExtractor.h\"\n#include \"../state/ActionInjector.h\"\n#include \"../headless/HeadlessGameEngine.h\"\n\n// Forward declare ZMQ types to avoid header pollution\nnamespace zmq {\n    class context_t;\n    class socket_t;\n}\n\n/**\n * TrainingServer — ZeroMQ REQ-REP server for Python training loop.\n *\n * Protocol (binary, packed structs):\n *   Python sends:  ActionCommand\n *   Server replies: GameStateObservation\n *\n * The server runs the training loop:\n *   while running:\n *     recv ActionCommand from Python\n *     inject action into engine\n *     step N ticks (decision_ticks = 10)\n *     extract state observation\n *     send GameStateObservation to Python\n */\nclass TrainingServer {\npublic:\n    explicit TrainingServer(HeadlessGameEngine* engine, int port = 5555);\n    ~TrainingServer();\n\n    // Initialize ZeroMQ and bind to port\n    Bool init();\n\n    // Run the training loop (blocks until Python sends a QUIT message)\n    void run();\n\n    // Ticks per decision step (matches AGENT.ticks_per_decision in Python config)\n    static const int DECISION_TICKS = 10;\n\nprivate:\n    HeadlessGameEngine* m_engine;\n    StateExtractor      m_extractor;\n    ActionInjector      m_injector;\n    int                 m_port;\n\n    zmq::context_t* m_context;\n    zmq::socket_t*  m_socket;\n\n    // Message type tags (first byte of every message)\n    enum MsgType : uint8_t {\n        MSG_ACTION    = 1,  // Python → server: ActionCommand\n        MSG_RESET     = 2,  // Python → server: reset game\n        MSG_QUIT      = 3,  // Python → server: shutdown\n        MSG_OBS       = 10, // server → Python: GameStateObservation\n        MSG_READY     = 11, // server → Python: ready for first action\n    };\n\n    void handleReset();\n    void handleAction(const ActionCommand& action, GameStateObservation& obs);\n};\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: Create TrainingServer.cpp**\n\n```cpp\n// src/engine/server/TrainingServer.cpp\n#include \"PreRTS.h\"\n#include \"TrainingServer.h\"\n#include <zmq.hpp>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <cstdio>\n\nTrainingServer::TrainingServer(HeadlessGameEngine* engine, int port)\n    : m_engine(engine)\n    , m_port(port)\n    , m_context(nullptr)\n    , m_socket(nullptr)\n{}\n\nTrainingServer::~TrainingServer() {\n    delete m_socket;\n    delete m_context;\n}\n\nBool TrainingServer::init() {\n    m_context = new zmq::context_t(1);\n    m_socket  = new zmq::socket_t(*m_context, zmq::socket_type::rep);\n\n    char addr[64];\n    snprintf(addr, sizeof(addr), \"tcp://*:%d\", m_port);\n    m_socket->bind(addr);\n\n    printf(\"[TrainingServer] Listening on port %d\\n\", m_port);\n    return TRUE;\n}\n\nvoid TrainingServer::run() {\n    // Send initial READY signal\n    uint8_t readyBuf[1] = { MSG_READY };\n    m_socket->send(zmq::buffer(readyBuf, 1), zmq::send_flags::none);\n\n    while (true) {\n        zmq::message_t msg;\n        auto result = m_socket->recv(msg, zmq::recv_flags::none);\n        if (!result.has_value()) continue;\n\n        const uint8_t* data = static_cast<const uint8_t*>(msg.data());\n        uint8_t msgType = data[0];\n\n        if (msgType == MSG_QUIT) {\n            printf(\"[TrainingServer] Received QUIT\\n\");\n            uint8_t ack[1] = { MSG_READY };\n            m_socket->send(zmq::buffer(ack, 1), zmq::send_flags::none);\n            break;\n        }\n\n        if (msgType == MSG_RESET) {\n            handleReset();\n            GameStateObservation obs;\n            m_extractor.extract(obs);\n            m_socket->send(zmq::buffer(&obs, sizeof(obs)), zmq::send_flags::none);\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        if (msgType == MSG_ACTION && msg.size() == 1 + sizeof(ActionCommand)) {\n            ActionCommand action;\n            memcpy(&action, data + 1, sizeof(ActionCommand));\n\n            GameStateObservation obs;\n            handleAction(action, obs);\n\n            m_socket->send(zmq::buffer(&obs, sizeof(obs)), zmq::send_flags::none);\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // Unknown message — send empty obs\n        GameStateObservation obs;\n        memset(&obs, 0, sizeof(obs));\n        m_socket->send(zmq::buffer(&obs, sizeof(obs)), zmq::send_flags::none);\n    }\n}\n\nvoid TrainingServer::handleReset() {\n    // TODO: restart game with fresh map\n    // For now, log the reset request\n    printf(\"[TrainingServer] Reset requested (frame %u)\\n\",\n           TheGameLogic ? TheGameLogic->getFrame() : 0);\n}\n\nvoid TrainingServer::handleAction(const ActionCommand& action, GameStateObservation& obs) {\n    // 1. Inject action\n    m_injector.inject(action);\n\n    // 2. Step DECISION_TICKS frames\n    for (int i = 0; i < DECISION_TICKS; i++) {\n        if (!m_engine->stepFrame()) break;\n    }\n\n    // 3. Extract resulting state\n    m_extractor.extract(obs);\n}\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: Wire TrainingServer into HeadlessMain.cpp**\n\nUpdate `HeadlessMain.cpp` to start the server after engine init:\n\n```cpp\n#include \"server/TrainingServer.h\"\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int port = (argc > 2) ? atoi(argv[2]) : 5555;\n\n    ApplicationHInstance = GetModuleHandle(NULL);\n    ApplicationHWnd = NULL;\n    _Module.Init(NULL, ApplicationHInstance);\n\n    HeadlessGameEngine* engine = new HeadlessGameEngine();\n    TheGameEngine = engine;\n    engine->init(argc, argv);\n\n    printf(\"[Headless] Engine initialized at frame %d\\n\",\n           TheGameLogic ? TheGameLogic->getFrame() : -1);\n\n    TrainingServer server(engine, port);\n    if (!server.init()) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"Failed to start TrainingServer\\n\");\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    server.run();  // blocks until Python sends QUIT\n\n    delete engine;\n    _Module.Term();\n    return 0;\n}\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 5: Commit**\n\n```bash\ngit add src/engine/server/ src/engine/headless/HeadlessMain.cpp\ngit commit -m \"feat(engine): ZeroMQ training server — step/observe/act protocol\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### Task 11: Python Client and Gym Environment\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `src/zh_copilot/engine/client.py`\n- Create: `src/zh_copilot/engine/env.py`\n- Create: `tests/test_engine_client.py`\n\nPython side of the ZeroMQ connection. Wraps the server as a Gym environment.\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Create src/zh_copilot/engine/client.py**\n\n```python\n# src/zh_copilot/engine/client.py\n\"\"\"ZeroMQ client for the headless ZH engine training server.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport struct\nimport subprocess\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport zmq\n\nfrom zh_copilot.config import AGENT, STATE, ENGINE\nfrom zh_copilot.state.game_state import Action, GameState, SpatialMap, Entity, PlayerState, GlobalState\n\n# Message type tags (must match TrainingServer.h)\nMSG_ACTION = 1\nMSG_RESET  = 2\nMSG_QUIT   = 3\nMSG_OBS    = 10\nMSG_READY  = 11\n\n# Observation struct layout (must match Protocol.h GameStateObservation)\nMAX_PLAYERS        = ENGINE.max_players\nMAX_ENTITIES       = STATE.max_entities\nSPATIAL_RES        = STATE.spatial_resolution\nSPATIAL_CHANNELS   = STATE.spatial_channels\nENTITY_FEATURE_DIM = STATE.entity_feature_dim\n\n# Struct format strings for packing/unpacking\n# ActionCommand: int action_type, int target_entity_idx, float target_x,\n#                float target_y, int modifier, int player_idx\nACTION_FMT = \"iiffii\"\nACTION_SIZE = struct.calcsize(ACTION_FMT)\n\n\nclass EngineClient:\n    \"\"\"Connects to the headless ZH engine server via ZeroMQ.\n\n    Usage:\n        client = EngineClient(host=\"localhost\", port=5555)\n        client.connect()\n        obs = client.reset()\n        while True:\n            action = policy.select_action(obs)\n            obs, reward, done, info = client.step(action)\n            if done:\n                obs = client.reset()\n        client.close()\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, host: str = \"localhost\", port: int = 5555):\n        self.host = host\n        self.port = port\n        self._context: Optional[zmq.Context] = None\n        self._socket: Optional[zmq.Socket] = None\n\n    def connect(self, timeout_ms: int = 10_000) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Connect to the engine server. Server must already be running.\"\"\"\n        self._context = zmq.Context()\n        self._socket = self._context.socket(zmq.REQ)\n        self._socket.setsockopt(zmq.RCVTIMEO, timeout_ms)\n        self._socket.connect(f\"tcp://{self.host}:{self.port}\")\n\n    def reset(self) -> GameState:\n        \"\"\"Reset the game and return initial observation.\"\"\"\n        self._socket.send(bytes([MSG_RESET]))\n        raw = self._socket.recv()\n        return self._parse_observation(raw)\n\n    def step(self, action: Action, player_idx: int = 1) -> tuple[GameState, float, bool, dict]:\n        \"\"\"Send action, step engine, return (obs, reward, done, info).\"\"\"\n        msg = self._pack_action(action, player_idx)\n        self._socket.send(msg)\n        raw = self._socket.recv()\n        obs = self._parse_observation(raw)\n        done = obs.global_state.game_time > 0 and obs.entities.count == 0\n        reward = self._compute_reward(obs)\n        return obs, reward, done, {}\n\n    def close(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Send quit message and close connection.\"\"\"\n        if self._socket:\n            self._socket.send(bytes([MSG_QUIT]))\n            self._socket.recv()  # ack\n            self._socket.close()\n        if self._context:\n            self._context.term()\n\n    def _pack_action(self, action: Action, player_idx: int) -> bytes:\n        header = bytes([MSG_ACTION])\n        target_idx  = action.target_entity_idx if action.target_entity_idx is not None else -1\n        target_x, target_y = action.target_position if action.target_position else (-1.0, -1.0)\n        payload = struct.pack(\n            ACTION_FMT,\n            action.action_type,\n            target_idx,\n            target_x,\n            target_y,\n            action.modifier,\n            player_idx,\n        )\n        return header + payload\n\n    def _parse_observation(self, raw: bytes) -> GameState:\n        \"\"\"Parse raw binary observation into GameState.\"\"\"\n        offset = 0\n\n        # Frame and game metadata (4 + 4 + 1 + 4 = 13 bytes)\n        frame = struct.unpack_from(\"I\", raw, offset)[0]; offset += 4\n        game_time = struct.unpack_from(\"f\", raw, offset)[0]; offset += 4\n        game_over = struct.unpack_from(\"?\", raw, offset)[0]; offset += 1\n        winner = struct.unpack_from(\"i\", raw, offset)[0]; offset += 4\n\n        # Players (MAX_PLAYERS * player_struct_size)\n        players = PlayerState.empty()\n        # (Parse player struct bytes — structure matches PlayerObservation in Protocol.h)\n        # Simplified: skip detailed parsing for now, fill from raw\n\n        # Entities\n        entities = Entity.empty_batch()\n\n        # Spatial map\n        spatial_flat = np.frombuffer(\n            raw,\n            dtype=np.float32,\n            count=SPATIAL_CHANNELS * SPATIAL_RES * SPATIAL_RES,\n            offset=len(raw) - SPATIAL_CHANNELS * SPATIAL_RES * SPATIAL_RES * 4,\n        ).copy()\n        spatial_data = spatial_flat.reshape(SPATIAL_CHANNELS, SPATIAL_RES, SPATIAL_RES)\n\n        global_state = GlobalState(game_time=game_time)\n\n        return GameState(\n            spatial=SpatialMap(data=spatial_data),\n            entities=entities,\n            players=players,\n            global_state=global_state,\n        )\n\n    def _compute_reward(self, obs: GameState) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Simple win/loss reward. Richer shaping added in training pipeline.\"\"\"\n        # Placeholder — real reward shaping is in training/reward.py\n        return 0.0\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Create src/zh_copilot/engine/env.py**\n\n```python\n# src/zh_copilot/engine/env.py\n\"\"\"Gym-compatible environment wrapping the headless ZH engine.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nfrom zh_copilot.engine.client import EngineClient\nfrom zh_copilot.state.game_state import Action, GameState\n\n\nclass ZHEnv:\n    \"\"\"Gym-style environment interface for ZH Copilot training.\n\n    Wraps EngineClient to provide a standard reset/step interface\n    compatible with PPO training loops.\n\n    Args:\n        host: Hostname of the engine server (default: localhost)\n        port: ZeroMQ port (default: 5555)\n        player_idx: Which player slot the agent controls (1-based, 0 = neutral)\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        host: str = \"localhost\",\n        port: int = 5555,\n        player_idx: int = 1,\n    ):\n        self._client = EngineClient(host=host, port=port)\n        self._player_idx = player_idx\n        self._connected = False\n\n    def connect(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Connect to the engine server. Must be called before reset().\"\"\"\n        self._client.connect()\n        self._connected = True\n\n    def reset(self) -> GameState:\n        \"\"\"Reset the game. Returns initial observation.\"\"\"\n        assert self._connected, \"Call connect() before reset()\"\n        return self._client.reset()\n\n    def step(self, action: Action) -> tuple[GameState, float, bool, dict]:\n        \"\"\"Execute action and return (obs, reward, done, info).\"\"\"\n        assert self._connected\n        return self._client.step(action, player_idx=self._player_idx)\n\n    def close(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Disconnect from engine server.\"\"\"\n        if self._connected:\n            self._client.close()\n            self._connected = False\n\n    def __enter__(self) -> ZHEnv:\n        self.connect()\n        return self\n\n    def __exit__(self, *args) -> None:\n        self.close()\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: Create tests/test_engine_client.py**\n\n```python\n# tests/test_engine_client.py\n\"\"\"\nIntegration tests for the engine client.\n\nThese tests require the headless engine server to be running:\n    zh_headless.exe \"C:\\\\Program Files\\\\ZeroHour\" 5555\n\nSkip if server is not available (for CI without engine).\n\"\"\"\n\nimport pytest\nimport struct\n\nfrom zh_copilot.engine.client import EngineClient, MSG_ACTION, ACTION_FMT\nfrom zh_copilot.engine.env import ZHEnv\nfrom zh_copilot.state.game_state import Action\n\n\ndef is_server_available(host: str = \"localhost\", port: int = 5555) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Check if engine server is reachable.\"\"\"\n    import zmq\n    ctx = zmq.Context()\n    sock = ctx.socket(zmq.REQ)\n    sock.setsockopt(zmq.RCVTIMEO, 500)\n    sock.connect(f\"tcp://{host}:{port}\")\n    try:\n        sock.send(b\"\\x03\")  # MSG_QUIT\n        sock.recv()\n        return True\n    except zmq.error.Again:\n        return False\n    finally:\n        sock.close()\n        ctx.term()\n\n\nrequires_engine = pytest.mark.skipif(\n    not is_server_available(),\n    reason=\"Engine server not running — start zh_headless.exe first\"\n)\n\n\ndef test_action_pack_format():\n    \"\"\"Action packing format matches Protocol.h struct layout.\"\"\"\n    action = Action(action_type=4, target_entity_idx=None, target_position=(100.0, 200.0))\n    client = EngineClient()\n    packed = client._pack_action(action, player_idx=1)\n    assert packed[0] == MSG_ACTION\n    unpacked = struct.unpack(ACTION_FMT, packed[1:])\n    assert unpacked[0] == 4     # action_type = ACT_MOVE\n    assert unpacked[1] == -1    # no target entity\n    assert unpacked[2] == pytest.approx(100.0)  # target_x\n    assert unpacked[3] == pytest.approx(200.0)  # target_y\n    assert unpacked[5] == 1     # player_idx\n\n\ndef test_noop_action_pack():\n    \"\"\"No-op action packs correctly.\"\"\"\n    action = Action.noop()\n    client = EngineClient()\n    packed = client._pack_action(action, player_idx=1)\n    assert packed[0] == MSG_ACTION\n    unpacked = struct.unpack(ACTION_FMT, packed[1:])\n    assert unpacked[0] == 20  # ACT_NOOP\n\n\n@requires_engine\ndef test_env_reset():\n    \"\"\"Engine server returns valid initial observation on reset.\"\"\"\n    with ZHEnv(port=5555) as env:\n        obs = env.reset()\n        assert obs is not None\n        assert obs.spatial.data.shape == (40, 128, 128)\n        assert obs.global_state.game_time == pytest.approx(0.0, abs=1.0)\n\n\n@requires_engine\ndef test_env_step_noop():\n    \"\"\"Stepping with no-op advances game time.\"\"\"\n    with ZHEnv(port=5555) as env:\n        obs0 = env.reset()\n        obs1, reward, done, info = env.step(Action.noop())\n        # After 10 ticks at 30fps, time should advance ~0.333s\n        assert obs1.global_state.game_time > obs0.global_state.game_time\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: Run unit tests (not integration tests)**\n\n```bash\ncd /Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/zh-copilot\nsource .venv/bin/activate\npython -m pytest tests/test_engine_client.py -v -k \"not requires_engine\"\n```\n\nExpected: `test_action_pack_format` and `test_noop_action_pack` pass. {
  "file_path": "/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/zh-copilot/plans/plan-3-ai-model.md",
  "content": "# Plan 3: AI Model Architecture\n\n> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.\n\n**Goal:** Implement the full ZH-Copilot neural network in PyTorch — Custom CNN (spatial), Transformer (entities), MLP (scalars), LSTM (temporal), Strategy Encoder, Action Decoder, and Value Head. Model runs on M4 Mac via MPS backend.\n\n**Architecture:** Five encoders fuse into an LSTM. The LSTM output feeds three heads: policy (action decoder), value (win probability), and strategy (game phase embedding). The action decoder is auto-regressive: action type → target pointer → spatial position. All hyperparameters come from `config.MODEL`.\n\n**Tech Stack:** PyTorch 2.x, MPS (Apple Silicon GPU), pytest\n\n---\n\n## File Structure\n\n```\nsrc/zh_copilot/model/\n├── __init__.py              # exports ZHCopilotModel + forward signature\n├── spatial_encoder.py       # Custom CNN: (B, C, H, W) → (B, cnn_output_dim)\n├── entity_encoder.py        # Transformer: (B, N, D) → (B, N, dim) + pooled (B, dim)\n├── scalar_encoder.py        # MLP: (B, scalar_dim) → (B, mlp_hidden)\n├── temporal.py              # LSTM: fused → (B, lstm_hidden) + hidden state\n├── strategy_encoder.py      # Strategy: lstm_out → (B, strategy_dim)\n├── action_decoder.py        # Action type + pointer + spatial heads\n├── value_head.py            # Win probability: (B, lstm_hidden) → (B, 1)\n└── zh_model.py              # ZHCopilotModel: wires everything together\n\ntests/model/\n├── __init__.py\n├── test_spatial_encoder.py\n├── test_entity_encoder.py\n├── test_scalar_encoder.py\n├── test_temporal.py\n├── test_action_decoder.py\n├── test_value_head.py\n└── test_zh_model.py\n```\n\n---\n\n### Task 1: Spatial Encoder (Custom CNN)\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `src/zh_copilot/model/spatial_encoder.py`\n- Create: `tests/model/test_spatial_encoder.py`\n- Create: `tests/model/__init__.py`\n\nInput: `(B, 40, 128, 128)` spatial map tensor\nOutput: `(B, 256)` feature vector\n\nArchitecture: 6 residual conv blocks with squeeze-excitation attention.\nProgressive downsampling: 128→64→32→16→8→4. Final global average pool → linear.\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Write failing tests**\n\n```python\n# tests/model/test_spatial_encoder.py\n\"\"\"Tests for the spatial map CNN encoder.\"\"\"\n\nimport torch\nimport pytest\nfrom zh_copilot.model.spatial_encoder import SpatialEncoder\nfrom zh_copilot.config import MODEL, STATE\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef encoder():\n    return SpatialEncoder(\n        in_channels=STATE.spatial_channels,\n        base_channels=MODEL.cnn_base_channels,\n        num_blocks=MODEL.cnn_blocks,\n        output_dim=MODEL.cnn_output_dim,\n    )\n\n\ndef test_output_shape(encoder):\n    x = torch.zeros(2, STATE.spatial_channels, STATE.spatial_resolution, STATE.spatial_resolution)\n    out = encoder(x)\n    assert out.shape == (2, MODEL.cnn_output_dim)\n\n\ndef test_output_dtype(encoder):\n    x = torch.zeros(2, STATE.spatial_channels, STATE.spatial_resolution, STATE.spatial_resolution)\n    out = encoder(x)\n    assert out.dtype == torch.float32\n\n\ndef test_single_batch(encoder):\n    x = torch.zeros(1, STATE.spatial_channels, STATE.spatial_resolution, STATE.spatial_resolution)\n    out = encoder(x)\n    assert out.shape == (1, MODEL.cnn_output_dim)\n\n\ndef test_different_inputs_produce_different_outputs(encoder):\n    x1 = torch.zeros(1, STATE.spatial_channels, STATE.spatial_resolution, STATE.spatial_resolution)\n    x2 = torch.ones(1, STATE.spatial_channels, STATE.spatial_resolution, STATE.spatial_resolution)\n    out1 = encoder(x1)\n    out2 = encoder(x2)\n    assert not torch.allclose(out1, out2)\n\n\ndef test_gradient_flows(encoder):\n    x = torch.randn(2, STATE.spatial_channels, STATE.spatial_resolution, STATE.spatial_resolution,\n                    requires_grad=True)\n    out = encoder(x)\n    loss = out.sum()\n    loss.backward()\n    assert x.grad is not None\n    assert x.grad.shape == x.shape\n\n\ndef test_parameter_count(encoder):\n    params = sum(p.numel() for p in encoder.parameters())\n    # Custom CNN should be well under 10M params for this input size\n    assert params < 10_000_000, f\"Too many params: {params:,}\"\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Run tests to verify they fail**\n\n```bash\ncd /Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/zh-copilot\nsource .venv/bin/activate\npip install torch --quiet\npython -m pytest tests/model/test_spatial_encoder.py -v\n```\nExpected: `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'zh_copilot.model.spatial_encoder'`\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: Create tests/model/__init__.py**\n\n```python\n# tests/model/__init__.py\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: Implement spatial_encoder.py**\n\n```python\n# src/zh_copilot/model/spatial_encoder.py\n\"\"\"Custom CNN encoder for the spatial game map.\n\nInput:  (B, C, H, W) — B=batch, C=spatial_channels (~40), H=W=128\nOutput: (B, output_dim) — pooled feature vector\n\nArchitecture:\n  6 residual blocks with squeeze-excitation attention.\n  Downsampling: 128 → 64 → 32 → 16 → 8 → 4\n  Final: global average pool → linear projection\n\"\"\"\n\nimport torch\nimport torch.nn as nn\nimport torch.nn.functional as F\n\n\nclass SqueezeExcitation(nn.Module):\n    \"\"\"Channel attention: re-weights channels based on global context.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, channels: int, reduction: int = 4):\n        super().__init__()\n        hidden = max(channels // reduction, 4)\n        self.fc = nn.Sequential(\n            nn.Linear(channels, hidden),\n            nn.ReLU(inplace=True),\n            nn.Linear(hidden, channels),\n            nn.Sigmoid(),\n        )\n\n    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\n        # x: (B, C, H, W)\n        w = x.mean(dim=(2, 3))          # (B, C) global average pool\n        w = self.fc(w).unsqueeze(-1).unsqueeze(-1)  # (B, C, 1, 1)\n        return x * w\n\n\nclass ResidualBlock(nn.Module):\n    \"\"\"Residual conv block with optional downsampling and SE attention.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, in_ch: int, out_ch: int, stride: int = 1):\n        super().__init__()\n        self.conv = nn.Sequential(\n            nn.Conv2d(in_ch, out_ch, kernel_size=3, stride=stride, padding=1, bias=False),\n            nn.BatchNorm2d(out_ch),\n            nn.ReLU(inplace=True),\n            nn.Conv2d(out_ch, out_ch, kernel_size=3, stride=1, padding=1, bias=False),\n            nn.BatchNorm2d(out_ch),\n        )\n        self.se = SqueezeExcitation(out_ch)\n        self.shortcut = nn.Sequential(\n            nn.Conv2d(in_ch, out_ch, kernel_size=1, stride=stride, bias=False),\n            nn.BatchNorm2d(out_ch),\n        ) if (stride != 1 or in_ch != out_ch) else nn.Identity()\n        self.relu = nn.ReLU(inplace=True)\n\n    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\n        return self.relu(self.se(self.conv(x)) + self.shortcut(x))\n\n\nclass SpatialEncoder(nn.Module):\n    \"\"\"Custom CNN for spatial map encoding.\n\n    Progressively downsamples 128×128 → 4×4 using 6 residual blocks.\n    Channels double every 2 blocks. Global average pool → linear projection.\n\n    Args:\n        in_channels:  Number of input channels (spatial_channels, ~40)\n        base_channels: Initial channel width (doubles per stage)\n        num_blocks:   Total residual blocks (must be even, default 6)\n        output_dim:   Output feature dimension (default 256)\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        in_channels: int = 40,\n        base_channels: int = 32,\n        num_blocks: int = 6,\n        output_dim: int = 256,\n    ):\n        super().__init__()\n        assert num_blocks % 2 == 0, \"num_blocks must be even\"\n\n        # Initial projection: in_channels → base_channels, no downsample\n        self.stem = nn.Sequential(\n            nn.Conv2d(in_channels, base_channels, kernel_size=3, padding=1, bias=False),\n            nn.BatchNorm2d(base_channels),\n            nn.ReLU(inplace=True),\n        )\n\n        # Build residual blocks: each pair doubles channels and halves spatial dims\n        blocks = []\n        in_ch = base_channels\n        for i in range(num_blocks):\n            out_ch = base_channels * (2 ** (i // 2))\n            stride = 2 if (i % 2 == 0 and i > 0) else 1  # downsample on even blocks\n            blocks.append(ResidualBlock(in_ch, out_ch, stride=stride))\n            in_ch = out_ch\n        self.blocks = nn.Sequential(*blocks)\n\n        # Global average pool → linear projection\n        self.pool = nn.AdaptiveAvgPool2d(1)\n        self.proj = nn.Linear(in_ch, output_dim)\n\n    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\n        # x: (B, C, H, W)\n        x = self.stem(x)\n        x = self.blocks(x)\n        x = self.pool(x).flatten(1)   # (B, final_channels)\n        x = self.proj(x)              # (B, output_dim)\n        return x\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 5: Run tests to verify they pass**\n\n```bash\npython -m pytest tests/model/test_spatial_encoder.py -v\n```\nExpected: all 6 tests PASS\n\n- [ ] **Step 6: out_all, pool_all = encoder(x, mask_all)\n\n    # Pooled output should differ (different number of valid entities)\n    assert not torch.allclose(pool_5, pool_all)\n\n\ndef test_single_entity(encoder):\n    B, N, D = 1, 1, STATE.entity_feature_dim\n    x = torch.randn(B, N, D)\n    mask = torch.ones(B, N, dtype=torch.bool)\n    per_entity, pooled = encoder(x, mask)\n    assert per_entity.shape == (B, N, MODEL.transformer_dim)\n    assert pooled.shape == (B, MODEL.transformer_dim)\n\n\ndef test_max_entities(encoder):\n    \"\"\"Should handle up to max_entities without assert per_entity.shape == (B, N, MODEL.transformer_dim)\n\n\ndef test_gradient_flows(encoder):\n    B, N, D = 2, 20, STATE.entity_feature_dim\n    x = torch.randn(B, N, D, requires_grad=True)\n    mask = torch.ones(B, N, dtype=torch.bool)\n    _, pooled = encoder(x, mask)\n    pooled.sum().backward()\n    assert x.grad is not None\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Run tests to verify they fail**\n\n```bash\npython -m pytest tests/model/test_entity_encoder.py -v\n```\nExpected: `ModuleNotFoundError`\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: Implement entity_encoder.py**\n\n```python\n# src/zh_copilot/model/entity_encoder.py\n\"\"\"Transformer encoder for the entity (unit/building) list.\n\nInput:\n  features: (B, N, D)  — per-entity feature vectors\n  mask:     (B, N)     — True where entity slot is occupied\n\nOutput:\n  per_entity: (B, N, model_dim)  — contextual embedding per entity\n  pooled:     (B, model_dim)     — masked mean pool over valid entities\n\nUses PyTorch's built-in MultiheadAttention for efficiency.\nPadding mask applied so invalid (empty) entity slots don't pollute attention.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport torch\nimport torch.nn as nn\nimport torch.nn.functional as F\n\n\nclass EntityEncoder(nn.Module):\n    \"\"\"Multi-layer Transformer encoder for the entity list.\n\n    Args:\n        feature_dim: Raw entity feature dimension (entity_feature_dim, 32)\n        model_dim:   Transformer hidden dimension (transformer_dim, 256)\n        num_layers:  Number of Transformer encoder layers (8)\n        num_heads:   Number of attention heads (8)\n        ff_dim:      Feed-forward hidden dim (default 4 × model_dim)\n        dropout:     Dropout rate (default 0.1)\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        feature_dim: int = 32,\n        model_dim: int = 256,\n        num_layers: int = 8,\n        num_heads: int = 8,\n        ff_dim: int | None = None,\n        dropout: float = 0.1,\n    ):\n        super().__init__()\n        ff_dim = ff_dim or model_dim * 4\n\n        # Project raw features into transformer dimension\n        self.input_proj = nn.Linear(feature_dim, model_dim)\n\n        # Stack of Transformer encoder layers\n        layer = nn.TransformerEncoderLayer(\n            d_model=model_dim,\n            nhead=num_heads,\n            dim_feedforward=ff_dim,\n            dropout=dropout,\n            batch_first=True,   # (B, N, D) convention\n            norm_first=True,    # pre-norm (more stable training)\n        )\n        self.transformer = nn.TransformerEncoder(layer, num_layers=num_layers)\n\n    def forward(\n        self,\n        features: torch.Tensor,   # (B, N, D)\n        mask: torch.Tensor,       # (B, N) bool — True where entity is valid\n    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Returns:\n            per_entity: (B, N, model_dim)\n            pooled:     (B, model_dim) — mean over valid entities\n        \"\"\"\n        # Project features\n        x = self.input_proj(features)   # (B, N, model_dim)\n\n        # PyTorch TransformerEncoder expects src_key_padding_mask where\n        # True = IGNORE (padding). Our mask is True = VALID, so invert.\n        padding_mask = ~mask   # (B, N) — True where slot is empty\n\n        # Run transformer (masked attention)\n        x = self.transformer(x, src_key_padding_mask=padding_mask)  # (B, N, model_dim)\n\n        # Masked mean pool over valid entities\n        valid = mask.unsqueeze(-1).float()           # (B, N, 1)\n        pooled = (x * valid).sum(dim=1)              # (B, model_dim)\n        count = valid.sum(dim=1).clamp(min=1.0)      # (B, 1) avoid div-by-zero\n        pooled = pooled / count\n\n        return x, pooled\n```\n\n- masked mean pool\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### Task 3: Scalar Encoder and Temporal Module\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `src/zh_copilot/model/scalar_encoder.py`\n- Create: `src/zh_copilot/model/temporal.py`\n- torch.randn(2, STATE.global_scalar_dim, requires_grad=True)\n    enc(x).sum().backward()\n    assert x.grad is not None\n\n\ndef test_temporal_output_shape():\n    mod = TemporalModule(input_dim=MODEL.fusion_dim, hidden_dim=MODEL.lstm_hidden)\n    x = torch.zeros(2, MODEL.fusion_dim)\n    out, hidden = mod(x, hidden=None)\n    assert out.shape == (2, MODEL.lstm_hidden)\n    assert hidden[0].shape == (1, 2, MODEL.lstm_hidden)  # (num_layers, B, hidden)\n    assert hidden[1].shape == (1, 2, MODEL.lstm_hidden)\n\n\ndef test_temporal_hidden_state_persists():\n    mod = TemporalModule(input_dim=MODEL.fusion_dim, hidden_dim=MODEL.lstm_hidden)\n    x = torch.randn(1, MODEL.fusion_dim)\n    out1, hidden1 = mod(x, hidden=None)\n    out2, hidden2 = mod(x, hidden=hidden1)\n    # Outputs should differ because hidden state changed\n    assert not torch.allclose(out1, out2)\n\n\ndef test_temporal_gradient_flows():\n out.sum().backward()\n    assert x.grad is not None\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Run tests to verify they fail**\n\n```bash\npython -m pytest tests/model/test_temporal.py -v\n```\nExpected: `ModuleNotFoundError`\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: Implement scalar_encoder.py**\n\n```python\n# src/zh_copilot/model/scalar_encoder.py\n\"\"\"Two-layer MLP encoder for global scalar game state.\n\nInput:  (B, scalar_dim) — concatenated per-player + game-level scalars\nOutput: (B, hidden_dim) — encoded feature vector\n\"\"\"\n\nimport torch\nimport torch.nn as nn\n\n\nclass ScalarEncoder(nn.Module):\n    \"\"\"Two-layer MLP with LayerNorm for global scalar state.\n\n    Args:\n        input_dim:  Number of input scalar features (global_scalar_dim, 80)\n        hidden_dim: Output feature dimension (global_mlp_hidden, 128)\n        dropout:    Dropout rate (default 0.1)\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, input_dim: int = 80, hidden_dim: int = 128, dropout: float = 0.1):\n        super().__init__()\n        self.net = nn.Sequential(\n            nn.Linear(input_dim, hidden_dim),\n            nn.LayerNorm(hidden_dim),\n            nn.ReLU(inplace=True),\n            nn.Dropout(dropout),\n            nn.Linear(hidden_dim, hidden_dim),\n            nn.LayerNorm(hidden_dim),\n            nn.ReLU(inplace=True),\n        )\n\n    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\n        return self.net(x)\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: Implement temporal.py**\n\n```python\n# src/zh_copilot/model/temporal.py\n\"\"\"LSTM temporal module — maintains game history context across decision steps.\n\nInput:  (B, input_dim) fused feature vector from CNN + Transformer + MLP\nOutput: (B, hidden_dim) LSTM output + new hidden state\n\nThe hidden state is passed across decision steps by the caller (training loop\nor inference engine) to give the model memory of past observations.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport torch\nimport torch.nn as nn\n\n\nclass TemporalModule(nn.Module):\n    \"\"\"Single-layer LSTM for temporal context.\n\n    Args:\n        input_dim:  Dimension of fused input (fusion_dim, 512)\n        hidden_dim: LSTM hidden state size (lstm_hidden, 512)\n        dropout:    Dropout on output (default 0.1)\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, input_dim: int = 512, hidden_dim: int = 512, dropout: float = 0.1):\n        super().__init__()\n        self.hidden_dim = hidden_dim\n        self.lstm = nn.LSTM(\n            input_size=input_dim,\n            hidden_size=hidden_dim,\n            num_layers=1,\n            batch_first=True,\n        )\n        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)\n\n    def forward(\n        self,\n        x: torch.Tensor,                               # (B, input_dim)\n        hidden: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,\n    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Args:\n            x:      (B, input_dim) — current fused features\n            hidden: (h_n, c_n) LSTM state from previous step, or None for zero init\n\n        Returns:\n            out:    (B, hidden_dim) — LSTM output for this step\n            hidden: (h_n, c_n) — updated LSTM state to pass to next step\n        \"\"\"\n        # LSTM expects (B, seq_len, input_dim) — seq_len = 1 per decision step\n        out, hidden = self.lstm(x.unsqueeze(1), hidden)  # out: (B, 1, hidden_dim)\n        out = self.dropout(out.squeeze(1))               # (B, hidden_dim)\n        return out, hidden\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 5: Run tests**\n\n```bash\npython -m pytest tests/model/test_temporal.py -v\n```\nExpected: all 5 tests PASS\n\n- [ ] **Step 6: Commit**\n\n```bash\ngit add src/zh_copilot/model/scalar_encoder.py src/zh_copilot/model/temporal.py softmax over 21 action types\n2. **Target entity** — pointer network (dot-product attention over entity embeddings)\n3. **Target position** — spatial probability map (64×64, deconvolved from LSTM
