entity_mask = torch.ones(2, 50, dtype=torch.bool)\n\n    # Pad entity_emb to max_entities\n    B, N = entity_emb.shape[:2]\n    full_emb = torch.zeros(B, STATE.max_entities, MODEL.transformer_dim)\n    full_emb[:, :N] = entity_emb\n    full_mask = torch.zeros(B, STATE.max_entities, dtype=torch.bool)\n    full_mask[:, :N] = True\n\n    logits, ptr_logits, spatial = decoder(lstm_out, full_emb, full_mask)\n    assert torch.isfinite(logits).all()\n    assert torch.isfinite(ptr_logits).all()\n    assert torch.isfinite(spatial).all()\n\n\ndef test_pointer_respects_mask(decoder):\n    \"\"\"Masked entities should have -inf pointer logits.\"\"\"\n    B = 1\n    lstm_out = torch.randn(B, MODEL.lstm_hidden)\n    entity_emb = torch.zeros(B, STATE.max_entities, MODEL.transformer_dim)\n    mask = torch.zeros(B, STATE.max_entities, dtype=torch.bool)\n    mask[:, :10] = True  # only first 10 valid\n\n    _, ptr_logits, _ = decoder(lstm_out, entity_emb, mask)\n    # Invalid entities should have very negative logits\n    assert (ptr_logits[:, 10:] < -1e4).all()\n\n\ndef test_value_head_shape(value):\n    lstm_out = torch.zeros(3, MODEL.lstm_hidden)\n    v = value(lstm_out)\n    assert v.shape == (3, 1)\n\n\ndef test_value_in_range(value):\n    lstm_out = torch.randn(10, MODEL.lstm_hidden)\n    v = value(lstm_out)\n    assert (v >= 0.0).all() and (v <= 1.0).all()\n\n\ndef test_gradients_flow_through_decoder(decoder):\n    lstm_out = torch.randn(2, MODEL.lstm_hidden, requires_grad=True)\n    entity_emb = torch.zeros(2, STATE.max_entities, MODEL.transformer_dim)\n    mask = torch.ones(2, STATE.max_entities, dtype=torch.bool)\n\n    logits, ptr_logits, spatial = decoder(lstm_out, entity_emb, mask)\n    (logits.sum() + ptr_logits.sum() + spatial.sum()).backward()\n    assert lstm_out.grad is not None\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Run tests to verify they fail**\n\n```bash\npython -m pytest tests/model/test_action_decoder.py -v\n```\nExpected: `ModuleNotFoundError`\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: Implement value_head.py**\n\n```python\n# src/zh_copilot/model/value_head.py\n\"\"\"Value head — estimates win probability from LSTM output.\n\nInput:  (B, lstm_hidden)\nOutput: (B, 1) — win probability in [0, 1]\n\nUsed by the actor-critic PPO training loop.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport torch\nimport torch.nn as nn\n\n\nclass ValueHead(nn.Module):\n    \"\"\"Two-layer MLP value head.\n\n    Args:\n        input_dim:  LSTM output dimension (lstm_hidden, 512)\n        hidden_dim: Hidden layer size (value_hidden, 256)\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, input_dim: int = 512, hidden_dim: int = 256):\n        super().__init__()\n        self.net = nn.Sequential(\n            nn.Linear(input_dim, hidden_dim),\n            nn.ReLU(inplace=True),\n            nn.Linear(hidden_dim, 1),\n            nn.Sigmoid(),   # output in [0, 1] = win probability\n        )\n\n    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\n        return self.net(x)\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: Implement action_decoder.py**\n\n```python\n# src/zh_copilot/model/action_decoder.py\n\"\"\"Auto-regressive action decoder.\n\nGiven LSTM output and entity embeddings, produces:\n  1. action_type_logits: (B, num_action_types) — what to do\n  2. pointer_logits:     (B, N)               — which entity to target\n  3. spatial_logits:     (B, R, R)            — where on the map\n\nAll outputs are raw logits (no softmax applied here — done at sampling time).\n\"\"\"\n\nimport torch\nimport torch.nn as nn\nimport torch.nn.functional as F\n\n\nclass ActionDecoder(nn.Module):\n    \"\"\"Auto-regressive action decoder.\n\n    Args:\n        lstm_dim:          LSTM output dimension (512)\n        entity_dim:        Transformer entity embedding dimension (256)\n        num_action_types:  Number of discrete action types (21)\n        spatial_resolution: Spatial output grid size (64)\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        lstm_dim: int = 512,\n        entity_dim: int = 256,\n        num_action_types: int = 21,\n        spatial_resolution: int = 64,\n    ):\n        super().__init__()\n        self.spatial_resolution = spatial_resolution\n\n        # 1. Action type head: LSTM → logits over action types\n        self.action_type_head = nn.Sequential(\n            nn.Linear(lstm_dim, lstm_dim // 2),\n            nn.ReLU(inplace=True),\n            nn.Linear(lstm_dim // 2, num_action_types),\n        )\n\n        # 2. Pointer network: dot-product attention\n        # Query = linear(lstm_out), Keys = entity embeddings\n        self.ptr_query = nn.Linear(lstm_dim, entity_dim)\n\n        # 3. Spatial head: deconvolve LSTM output → (R, R) probability map\n        # LSTM → (8, 8) → upsample to (64, 64)\n        init_spatial = 8\n        self.spatial_head = nn.Sequential(\n            nn.Linear(lstm_dim, init_spatial * init_spatial * 64),\n            nn.ReLU(inplace=True),\n        )\n        self.spatial_upsample = nn.Sequential(\n            nn.ConvTranspose2d(64, 32, kernel_size=4, stride=2, padding=1),  # 8→16\n            nn.ReLU(inplace=True),\n            nn.ConvTranspose2d(32, 16, kernel_size=4, stride=2, padding=1),  # 16→32\n            nn.ReLU(inplace=True),\n            nn.ConvTranspose2d(16, 1,  kernel_size=4, stride=2, padding=1),  # 32→64\n        )\n        self._init_spatial = init_spatial\n\n    def forward(\n        self,\n        lstm_out: torch.Tensor,      # (B, lstm_dim)\n        entity_emb: torch.Tensor,    # (B, N, entity_dim)\n        entity_mask: torch.Tensor,   # (B, N) bool — True where valid\n    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Returns:\n            action_logits:  (B, num_action_types) — raw logits\n            pointer_logits: (B, N) — raw logits (masked invalid → -inf)\n            spatial_logits: (B, R, R) — raw logits (R = spatial_resolution)\n        \"\"\"\n        B = lstm_out.shape[0]\n        N = entity_emb.shape[1]\n\n        # 1. Action type logits\n        action_logits = self.action_type_head(lstm_out)  # (B, num_action_types)\n\n        # 2. Pointer network\n        query = self.ptr_query(lstm_out).unsqueeze(1)    # (B, 1, entity_dim)\n        # Scaled dot-product: (B, 1, entity_dim) × (B, entity_dim, N) → (B, 1, N)\n        scale = entity_emb.shape[-1] ** 0.5\n        ptr_logits = torch.bmm(query, entity_emb.transpose(1, 2)).squeeze(1) / scale  # (B, N)\n        # Mask invalid entities: set to -inf so they can't be selected\n        ptr_logits = ptr_logits.masked_fill(~entity_mask, float(\"-inf\"))\n\n        # 3. Spatial head\n        s = self.spatial_head(lstm_out)                               # (B, 8*8*64)\n        s = s.view(B, 64, self._init_spatial, self._init_spatial)    # (B, 64, 8, 8)\n        s = self.spatial_upsample(s).squeeze(1)                      # (B, R, R)\n\n        return action_logits, ptr_logits, s\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 5: Run tests**\n\n```bash\npython -m pytest tests/model/test_action_decoder.py -v\n```\nExpected: all 6 tests PASS\n\n- [ ] **Step 6: Commit**\n\n```bash\ngit add src/zh_copilot/model/action_decoder.py src/zh_copilot/model/value_head.py tests/model/test_action_decoder.py\ngit commit -m \"feat(model): action decoder (pointer + spatial) and value head\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### Task 5: Strategy Encoder\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `src/zh_copilot/model/strategy_encoder.py`\n\nInput: LSTM output + human strategy override (one-hot)\nOutput: `(B, strategy_dim)` strategy embedding\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Write test (inline)**\n\n```python\n# Add to tests/model/test_action_decoder.py or new file tests/model/test_strategy.py\n\n# tests/model/test_strategy.py\nimport torch\nfrom zh_copilot.model.strategy_encoder import StrategyEncoder\nfrom zh_copilot.config import MODEL, STATE\n\n\ndef test_strategy_output_shape():\n    enc = StrategyEncoder(\n        lstm_dim=MODEL.lstm_hidden,\n        strategy_dim=MODEL.strategy_dim,\n        num_buttons=STATE.num_strategy_buttons,\n    )\n    lstm_out = torch.zeros(2, MODEL.lstm_hidden)\n    override = torch.zeros(2, STATE.num_strategy_buttons)\n    out = enc(lstm_out, override)\n    assert out.shape == (2, MODEL.strategy_dim)\n\n\ndef test_strategy_responds_to_override():\n    enc = StrategyEncoder(\n        lstm_dim=MODEL.lstm_hidden,\n        strategy_dim=MODEL.strategy_dim,\n        num_buttons=STATE.num_strategy_buttons,\n    )\n    lstm_out = torch.randn(1, MODEL.lstm_hidden)\n    override_auto   = torch.zeros(1, STATE.num_strategy_buttons)\n    override_auto[:, 5] = 1.0   # \"auto\" button\n    override_attack = torch.zeros(1, STATE.num_strategy_buttons)\n    override_attack[:, 0] = 1.0  # \"attack\" button\n\n    out_auto   = enc(lstm_out, override_auto)\n    out_attack = enc(lstm_out, override_attack)\n    assert not torch.allclose(out_auto, out_attack)\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Run tests to verify they fail**\n\n```bash\npython -m pytest tests/model/test_strategy.py -v\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: Implement strategy_encoder.py**\n\n```python\n# src/zh_copilot/model/strategy_encoder.py\n\"\"\"Strategy encoder — produces a continuous strategy embedding.\n\nCombines LSTM context with the human copilot override signal\n(one-hot over 6 strategy buttons: attack, defend, rush, farm, turtle, auto).\n\nOutput feeds back into the policy head as conditioning.\nThe model learns strategic archetypes from replays — not hardcoded plans.\n\nInput:\n    lstm_out:         (B, lstm_dim)        — current game context\n    strategy_override: (B, num_buttons)    — one-hot human signal (or zeros for auto)\n\nOutput: (B, strategy_dim) — continuous strategy embedding\n\"\"\"\n\nimport torch\nimport torch.nn as nn\n\n\nclass StrategyEncoder(nn.Module):\n    \"\"\"Strategy encoder combining LSTM context and human override.\n\n    Args:\n        lstm_dim:     LSTM output dimension (512)\n        strategy_dim: Output strategy embedding dimension (64)\n        num_buttons:  Number of copilot UI buttons (6)\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        lstm_dim: int = 512,\n        strategy_dim: int = 64,\n        num_buttons: int = 6,\n    ):\n        super().__init__()\n        self.net = nn.Sequential(\n            nn.Linear(lstm_dim + num_buttons, strategy_dim * 2),\n            nn.ReLU(inplace=True),\n            nn.Linear(strategy_dim * 2, strategy_dim),\n            nn.Tanh(),   # bound to [-1, 1] — stable conditioning signal\n        )\n\n    def forward(\n        self,\n        lstm_out: torch.Tensor,          # (B, lstm_dim)\n        strategy_override: torch.Tensor, # (B, num_buttons)\n    ) -> torch.Tensor:                   # (B, strategy_dim)\n        x = torch.cat([lstm_out, strategy_override], dim=-1)\n        return self.net(x)\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: Run tests**\n\n```bash\npython -m pytest tests/model/test_strategy.py -v\n```\nExpected: both tests PASS\n\n- [ ] **Step 5: Commit**\n\n```bash\ngit add src/zh_copilot/model/strategy_encoder.py tests/model/test_strategy.py\ngit commit -m \"feat(model): strategy encoder — learned archetypes with human override conditioning\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### Task 6: Full Model Integration (ZHCopilotModel)\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `src/zh_copilot/model/zh_model.py`\n- Update: `src/zh_copilot/model/__init__.py`\n- Create: `tests/model/test_zh_model.py`\n\nWires all components together. Implement zh_model.py**\n\n```python\n# src/zh_copilot/model/zh_model.py\n\"\"\"ZHCopilotModel — full model wiring all components together.\n\nForward pass:\n  spatial map  → SpatialEncoder  → (B, 256)\n  entities     → EntityEncoder   → (B, 256)\n  scalars      → ScalarEncoder   → (B, 128)\n                 → cat + Fusion MLP → (B, 512)\n                 → TemporalModule (LSTM) → (B, 512)\n                 → StrategyEncoder → (B, 64)\n                 → ActionDecoder → logits\n                 → ValueHead → value\n\nAll hyperparameters from config.MODEL and config.STATE.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport torch\nimport torch.nn as nn\n\nfrom zh_copilot.config import MODEL, STATE\nfrom zh_copilot.model.spatial_encoder import SpatialEncoder\nfrom zh_copilot.model.entity_encoder import EntityEncoder\nfrom zh_copilot.model.scalar_encoder import ScalarEncoder\nfrom zh_copilot.model.temporal import TemporalModule\nfrom zh_copilot.model.strategy_encoder import StrategyEncoder\nfrom zh_copilot.model.action_decoder import ActionDecoder\nfrom zh_copilot.model.value_head import ValueHead\n\n\nclass ZHCopilotModel(nn.Module):\n    \"\"\"Full ZH-Copilot policy and value network.\n\n    Input batch dict:\n        spatial:            (B, spatial_channels, H, W)\n        entity_features:    (B, max_entities, entity_feature_dim)\n        entity_mask:        (B, max_entities) bool\n        global_scalars:     (B, global_scalar_dim)\n        strategy_override:  (B, num_strategy_buttons) one-hot\n\n    Output dict:\n        action_logits:  (B, num_action_types)\n        ptr_logits:     (B, max_entities)\n        spatial_logits: (B, spatial_action_resolution, spatial_action_resolution)\n        value:          (B, 1) in [0, 1]\n        strategy_emb:   (B, strategy_dim)\n        hidden:         (h_n, c_n) LSTM state for next step\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self):\n        super().__init__()\n\n        # Encoders\n        self.spatial_enc = SpatialEncoder(\n            in_channels=STATE.spatial_channels,\n            base_channels=MODEL.cnn_base_channels,\n            num_blocks=MODEL.cnn_blocks,\n            output_dim=MODEL.cnn_output_dim,\n        )\n        self.entity_enc = EntityEncoder(\n            feature_dim=STATE.entity_feature_dim,\n            model_dim=MODEL.transformer_dim,\n            num_layers=MODEL.transformer_layers,\n            num_heads=MODEL.transformer_heads,\n        )\n        self.scalar_enc = ScalarEncoder(\n            input_dim=STATE.global_scalar_dim,\n            hidden_dim=MODEL.global_mlp_hidden,\n        )\n\n        # Fusion: concatenate all encoder outputs → project to fusion_dim\n        fused_dim = MODEL.cnn_output_dim + MODEL.transformer_dim + MODEL.global_mlp_hidden\n        self.fusion = nn.Sequential(\n            nn.Linear(fused_dim, MODEL.fusion_dim),\n            nn.LayerNorm(MODEL.fusion_dim),\n            nn.ReLU(inplace=True),\n        )\n\n        # Temporal\n        self.temporal = TemporalModule(\n            input_dim=MODEL.fusion_dim,\n            hidden_dim=MODEL.lstm_hidden,\n        )\n\n        # Heads\n        self.strategy_enc = StrategyEncoder(\n            lstm_dim=MODEL.lstm_hidden,\n            strategy_dim=MODEL.strategy_dim,\n            num_buttons=STATE.num_strategy_buttons,\n        )\n        self.action_decoder = ActionDecoder(\n            lstm_dim=MODEL.lstm_hidden + MODEL.strategy_dim,  # conditioned on strategy\n            entity_dim=MODEL.transformer_dim,\n            num_action_types=MODEL.num_action_types,\n            spatial_resolution=MODEL.spatial_action_resolution,\n        )\n        self.value_head = ValueHead(\n            input_dim=MODEL.lstm_hidden,\n            hidden_dim=MODEL.value_hidden,\n        )\n\n    def forward(\n        self,\n        batch: dict[str, torch.Tensor],\n        hidden: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None,\n    ) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor | tuple]:\n        # --- Encode ---\n        spatial_feat = self.spatial_enc(batch[\"spatial\"])               # (B, 256)\n        entity_emb, entity_pool = self.entity_enc(\n            batch[\"entity_features\"], batch[\"entity_mask\"]\n        )                                                                # (B, N, 256), (B, 256)\n        scalar_feat = self.scalar_enc(batch[\"global_scalars\"])          # (B, 128)\n\n        # --- Fuse ---\n        fused = torch.cat([spatial_feat, entity_pool, scalar_feat], dim=-1)  # (B, 640)\n        fused = self.fusion(fused)                                            # (B, 512)\n\n        # --- Temporal ---\n        lstm_out, new_hidden = self.temporal(fused, hidden)             # (B, 512)\n\n        # --- Strategy ---\n        strategy_emb = self.strategy_enc(lstm_out, batch[\"strategy_override\"])  # (B, 64)\n\n        # --- Action decoder (conditioned on strategy) ---\n        lstm_with_strategy = torch.cat([lstm_out, strategy_emb], dim=-1)  # (B, 576)\n        action_logits, ptr_logits, spatial_logits = self.action_decoder(\n            lstm_with_strategy, entity_emb, batch[\"entity_mask\"]\n        )\n\n        # --- Value head ---\n        value = self.value_head(lstm_out)                               # (B, 1)\n\n        return {\n            \"action_logits\":  action_logits,\n            \"ptr_logits\":     ptr_logits,\n            \"spatial_logits\": spatial_logits,\n            \"value\":          value,\n            \"strategy_emb\":   strategy_emb,\n            \"hidden\":         new_hidden,\n        }\n\n    def count_parameters(self) -> int:\n        return sum(p.numel() for p in self.parameters() if p.requires_grad)\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: Update model/__init__.py**\n\n```python\n# ## C&C Generals Zero Hour `.rep` Replay File Format — Complete Research Report

All findings below are sourced directly from the EA source code at `/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/zh-copilot/cczh-source-code/GeneralsMD/Code/GameEngine/Source/Common/Recorder.cpp` and supporting headers, supplemented by community research.

---

### Key Source Files

- `/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/zh-copilot/cczh-source-code/GeneralsMD/Code/GameEngine/Include/Common/Recorder.h` — `RecorderClass`, `ReplayHeader` struct
- `/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/zh-copilot/cczh-source-code/GeneralsMD/Code/GameEngine/Source/Common/Recorder.cpp` — all read/write logic
- `/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/zh-copilot/cczh-source-code/GeneralsMD/Code/GameEngine/Include/Common/MessageStream.h` — `GameMessage::Type` enum, argument types
- `/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/zh-copilot/cczh-source-code/GeneralsMD/Code/Libraries/Include/Lib/BaseType.h` — all primitive typedefs and coordinate structs

---

### Primitive Type Sizes (32-bit Windows build)

| C++ type | Size |
|---|---|
| `Bool` (`bool`) | 1 byte |
| `Int` (`int`) | 4 bytes |
| `UnsignedInt` | 4 bytes |
| `UnsignedByte` | 1 byte |
| `UnsignedShort` | 2 bytes |
| `Real` (`float`) | 4 bytes |
| `WideChar` (`wchar_t`) | 2 bytes |
| `time_t` | 4 bytes (32-bit build), 8 bytes (64-bit) |
| `ObjectID` / `DrawableID` / `teamID` | all `UnsignedInt` = 4 bytes |

**Important:** The game was compiled as a 32-bit Windows application. `time_t` is 4 bytes. All integers are little-endian.

---

### Part 1: File Header

The header is written by `startRecording()` and read by `readReplayHeader()`. The fixed-offset portion comes first, followed by variable-length strings.

#### Fixed-offset section (all offsets from file start)

| Offset | Field | Type/Size | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `0x000` | Magic | 6 bytes ASCII | `"GENREP"` — no null terminator |
| `0x006` | `startTime` | `time_t` = 4 bytes | Unix timestamp; filled in at game start via `fseek` back to `startTimeOffset = 6` |
| `0x00A` | `endTime` | `time_t` = 4 bytes | Unix timestamp; filled in at game end via `fseek` back to `endTimeOffset = 10` |
| `0x00E` | `frameDuration` | `UnsignedInt` = 4 bytes | Game logic frames at end of match; filled in at end via `fseek` to `framesOffset = 14` |
| `0x012` | `desyncGame` | `Bool` = 1 byte | `1` if a CRC mismatch occurred, patched in via `fseek` to `desyncOffset = 18` |
| `0x013` | `quitEarly` | `Bool` = 1 byte | `1` if game quit before natural end, patched at `quitEarlyOffset = 19` |
| `0x014` | `playerDiscons[0..7]` | 8 × `Bool` = 8 bytes | One flag per slot; patched individually at `disconOffset + slot` = `20 + slot` |

**Total fixed section: 28 bytes** (`0x000`–`0x01B`)

#### Variable-length section (immediately after offset `0x01C`)

All offsets from here are variable because strings have unknown lengths.

| Field | Type | Encoding |
|---|---|---|
| `replayName` | variable | Null-terminated UTF-16LE wide string; written with `fwprintf(m_file, L"%ws", ...)` + `fputwc(0, m_file)` |
| `timeVal` | `SYSTEMTIME` = 16 bytes | Win32 struct: 8 × `WORD` (year, month, dayOfWeek, day, hour, minute, second, milliseconds) |
| `versionString` | variable | Null-terminated UTF-16LE |
| `versionTimeString` | variable | Null-terminated UTF-16LE (build timestamp string) |
| `versionNumber` | `UnsignedInt` = 4 bytes | Packed version integer |
| `exeCRC` | `UnsignedInt` = 4 bytes | CRC of the game executable |
| `iniCRC` | `UnsignedInt` = 4 bytes | CRC of all INI data |
| `gameOptions` | variable | Null-terminated **ASCII** string — output of `GameInfoToAsciiString()`: encodes map, slots, colors, factions, teams, starting spots, CRC interval, etc. |
| `localPlayerIndex` | variable | Null-terminated **ASCII** string containing a decimal integer (e.g. `"0"`, `"-1"`) |
| `difficulty` | `Int` = 4 bytes | `GameDifficulty` enum |
| `originalGameMode` | `Int` = 4 bytes | `GAME_REPLAY`, `GAME_SKIRMISH`, etc. |
| `rankPoints` | `Int` = 4 bytes | Rank points at game start |
| `maxFPS` | `Int` = 4 bytes | FPS cap chosen by host |

After the last 4-byte field the command stream begins immediately.

---

### Part 2: Command Stream (Body)

Each command record is written by `writeToFile()` and read by `appendNextCommand()`. There is no length-prefix on the record — you must parse forward knowing the argument schema.

#### Per-command record layout

| Field | Type/Size | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `frame` | `UnsignedInt` = 4 bytes | Logic frame this command executes on |
| `type` | `GameMessage::Type` = 4 bytes | Command type enum value (see below) |
| `playerIndex` | `Int` = 4 bytes | Which player issued this command (0–7) |
| `numTypes` | `UnsignedByte` = 1 byte | Number of argument-type descriptors that follow |
| (repeated `numTypes` times:) | | |
| — `argType` | `UnsignedByte` = 1 byte | `GameMessageArgumentDataType` enum value (0–10) |
| — `argCount` | `UnsignedByte` = 1 byte | How many arguments of this type |
| (arguments, raw binary, as described by the type table) | | |

#### Argument data sizes (from `writeArgument` / `readArgument`)

| `GameMessageArgumentDataType` | Enum value | Size in file |
|---|---|---|
| `ARGUMENTDATATYPE_INTEGER` | 0 | 4 bytes (`Int`) |
| `ARGUMENTDATATYPE_REAL` | 1 | 4 bytes (`float`) |
| `ARGUMENTDATATYPE_BOOLEAN` | 2 | 1 byte (`Bool`) |
| `ARGUMENTDATATYPE_OBJECTID` | 3 | 4 bytes (`UnsignedInt`) |
| `ARGUMENTDATATYPE_DRAWABLEID` | 4 | 4 bytes (`UnsignedInt`) |
| `ARGUMENTDATATYPE_TEAMID` | 5 | 4 bytes (`UnsignedInt`) |
| `ARGUMENTDATATYPE_LOCATION` | 6 | 12 bytes (`Coord3D`: 3 × `float`) |
| `ARGUMENTDATATYPE_PIXEL` | 7 | 8 bytes (`ICoord2D`: 2 × `Int`) |
| `ARGUMENTDATATYPE_PIXELREGION` | 8 | 16 bytes (`IRegion2D`: 2 × `ICoord2D` = 4 × `Int`) |
| `ARGUMENTDATATYPE_TIMESTAMP` | 9 | 4 bytes (`UnsignedInt`) |
| `ARGUMENTDATATYPE_WIDECHAR` | 10 | 2 bytes (`wchar_t`) |
| `ARGUMENTDATATYPE_UNKNOWN` | 11 | 0 bytes (sentinel/skip) |

**Parsing algorithm:** Read the `numTypes` descriptor pairs. For each pair `(argType, argCount)`, read `argCount` raw values of the corresponding size. Total arguments = sum of all `argCount` values across all descriptor pairs.

---

### Part 3: End of File / Termination

There is no explicit file footer. The stream ends when `fread` on a `frame` value returns 0 bytes — `readNextFrame()` detects this and sets `m_nextFrame = -1`, stopping playback. The last record written before the stream ends is a `MSG_CLEAR_GAME_DATA` command.

---

### Part 4: Network Message Range

Only messages in the range `MSG_BEGIN_NETWORK_MESSAGES` (value **1000**) to `MSG_END_NETWORK_MESSAGES` (value **1999**) are written to the file. The full list of recorded command types starts at `MSG_BEGIN_NETWORK_MESSAGES = 1000`. Notable entries (from `MessageStream.h`):

| Value | Name |
|---|---|
| 1000 | `MSG_BEGIN_NETWORK_MESSAGES` (also the CRC broadcast message) |
| 1001 | `MSG_CREATE_SELECTED_GROUP` |
| 1002 | `MSG_CREATE_SELECTED_GROUP_NO_SOUND` |
| 1056 | `MSG_DO_MOVETO` |
| 1057 | `MSG_DO_ATTACKMOVETO` |
| 1075 | `MSG_LOGIC_CRC` |
| 1079 | `MSG_ENABLE_RETALIATION_MODE` |
| 1900 | `MSG_BEGIN_DEBUG_NETWORK_MESSAGES` |
| 1999 | `MSG_END_NETWORK_MESSAGES` |

`MSG_CLEAR_GAME_DATA` (value **10**, outside the 1000–1999 range) is written as the terminal record.

---

### Part 5: String Encoding Summary

| Location | Encoding |
|---|---|
| Magic signature | 6-byte ASCII, no terminator |
| `replayName`, `versionString`, `versionTimeString` | Null-terminated UTF-16LE (2 bytes/char + 2-byte null) |
| `gameOptions`, `localPlayerIndex` | Null-terminated ASCII (1 byte/char + 1-byte null) |

Strings are written with `fwprintf`/`fputwc(0)` (wide) or `fwrite`/`fputc(0)` (narrow). The game uses `fgetwc` to read wide strings and `fgetc` for narrow — meaning it reads character by character until it hits a `0` codepoint.

---

### Part 6: Community Parsers and References

Three well-regarded external implementations exist that confirm and extend the above:

1. **louisdx/cnc-replayreaders** — C++ dissector specifically for Generals/ZH in `ccgzhreader.cpp`; format doc in `eareplay.html`. The earliest and most authoritative community reverse-engineering.
   - https://github.com/louisdx/cnc-replayreaders

2. **Tim Jones / OpenSAGE blog post (2018)** — The definitive write-up on the format, confirms `"GENREP"` magic, header layout, and chunk structure. Jones implemented a C# parser in OpenSAGE.
   - http://timjones.io/blog/archive/2018/03/06/parsing-command-and-conquer-generals-replay-files
   - https://opensage.github.io/blog/replay-file-parsing/

3. **dsalzner/cnc-generals-replay-parser** — Python parser, tested against Zero Hour. Includes `packets.py` with a `buildingTypeMap` and a `formatLiveReplayHeader` function. Practical note: the header length can vary by game config.
   - https://github.com/dsalzner/cnc-generals-replay-parser/

4. **TheSuperHackers/GeneralsGameCode wiki** — replay_testing notes from the open-source re-release community
   - https://github.com/TheSuperHackers/GeneralsGameCode/wiki/replay_testing

---

### Practical Notes for Your Python Parser

- **Endianness:** All multi-byte integers are little-endian (x86 native).
- **`time_t` size:** Use 4 bytes for authentic ZH replays (compiled as Win32). If you ever encounter 64-bit builds, `time_t` will be 8 bytes and all fixed offsets shift by 8.
- **`MAX_SLOTS = 8`** (players 0–7), so `playerDiscons` is always exactly 8 bytes.
- **`SYSTEMTIME` = 16 bytes** (8 `WORD`s: year, month, dayOfWeek, day, hour, minute, second, milliseconds).
- **Wide strings** should be decoded as UTF-16LE with `str.decode('utf-16-le')` after stripping the null terminator.
- **`gameOptions` string** is the `GameInfoToAsciiString` format — a structured ASCII text block encoding map path, slot colors, factions, teams, etc. You'll need a sub-parser for it to extract player info.
- **Frame rate:** The game runs at 5 logic frames per second (30 Hz display / 6), so `frameDuration / 5` gives approximate game duration in seconds.
- **The CRC message** (`MSG_BEGIN_NETWORK_MESSAGES = 1000`) appears periodically (every 100 frames by default, `REPLAY_CRC_INTERVAL = 100`) with a single `INTEGER` argument holding the game state CRC. You can skip these for gameplay analysis.

Sources:
- [Parsing Command & Conquer: Generals replay files - timjones.io](http://timjones.io/blog/archive/2018/03/06/parsing-command-and-conquer-generals-replay-files)
- [Parsing Command & Conquer: Generals replay files - OpenSAGE blog](https://opensage.github.io/blog/replay-file-parsing/)
- [GitHub - louisdx/cnc-replayreaders](https://github.com/louisdx/cnc-replayreaders)
- [GitHub - dsalzner/cnc-generals-replay-parser](https://github.com/dsalzner/cnc-generals-replay-parser/)
- [replay_testing - TheSuperHackers/GeneralsGameCode Wiki](https://github.com/TheSuperHackers/GeneralsGameCode/wiki/replay_testing)

— minimal valid string\n    buf.write(b\"M=maps/alpine.map C=1 F=America C=2 F=China\\x00\")\n\n    # local_player_index (ASCII null-terminated)\n    buf.write(b\"0\\x00\")\n\n    # difficulty, original_game_mode, rank_points, max_fps\n    buf.write(struct.pack(\"<4i\", 1, 7, 0, 30))  # normal, skirmish, 0 pts, 30fps\n\n    # One command: MSG_DO_MOVETO at frame 30, player 0\n    # Args: one LOCATION (Coord3D) = 3 floats\n    buf.write(struct.pack(\"<I\", 30))          # frame\n    buf.write(struct.pack(\"<I\", MsgType.DO_MOVETO))  # type\n    buf.write(struct.pack(\"<i\", 0))           # player_idx\n    buf.write(struct.pack(\"<B\", 1))           # num_types = 1\n    buf.write(struct.pack(\"<BB\", ArgType.LOCATION, 1))  # argtype=LOCATION, count=1\n    buf.write(struct.pack(\"<3f\", 100.0, 200.0, 0.0))    # Coord3D\n\n    return buf.getvalue()\n\n\ndef test_magic_detection():\n    data = make_minimal_replay()\n    replay = parse_replay_bytes(data)\n    assert replay.header.magic == \"GENREP\"\n\n\ndef test_header_fixed_fields():\n    data = make_minimal_replay()\n    replay = parse_replay_bytes(data)\n    h = replay.header\n    assert h.start_time == 1700000000\n    assert h.end_time   == 1700003600\n    assert h.frame_duration == 9000\n    assert h.desync_game is False\n    assert h.quit_early  is False\n    assert all(d is False for d in h.player_discons)\n\n\ndef test_header_variable_strings():\n    data = make_minimal_replay()\n    replay = parse_replay_bytes(data)\n    h = replay.header\n    assert h.replay_name == \"Test Replay\"\n    assert h.version_string == \"1.04\"\n    assert h.version_number == 10401\n\n\ndef test_header_scalars():\n    data = make_minimal_replay()\n    replay = parse_replay_bytes(data)\n    h = replay.header\n    assert h.difficulty == 1\n    assert h.original_game_mode == 7\n    assert h.max_fps == 30\n\n\ndef test_command_parsed():\n    data = make_minimal_replay()\n    replay = parse_replay_bytes(data)\n    assert len(replay.commands) == 1\n    cmd = replay.commands[0]\n    assert cmd.frame == 30\n    assert cmd.msg_type == MsgType.DO_MOVETO\n    assert cmd.player_idx == 0\n\n\ndef test_command_location_argument():\n    data = make_minimal_replay()\n    replay = parse_replay_bytes(data)\n    cmd = replay.commands[0]\n    assert ArgType.LOCATION in cmd.args\n    locs = cmd.args[ArgType.LOCATION]\n    assert len(locs) == 1\n    assert locs[0] == pytest.approx((100.0, 200.0, 0.0))\n\n\ndef test_invalid_magic_raises():\n    data = bytearray(make_minimal_replay())\n    data[0:6] = b\"BADMAG\"\n    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=\"magic\"):\n        parse_replay_bytes(bytes(data))\n\n\ndef test_empty_file_raises():\n    with pytest.raises(ValueError):\n        parse_replay_bytes(b\"\")\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Run tests to verify they fail**\n\n```bash\ncd /Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/zh-copilot\nsource .venv/bin/activate\npython -m pytest parser.py**\n\n```python\n# src/zh_copilot/replay/parser.py\n\"\"\"Binary parser for C&C Generals Zero Hour .rep replay files.\n\nFormat documented in:\n  cczh-source-code/GeneralsMD/Code/GameEngine/Source/Common/Recorder.cpp\n\nUsage:\n    replay = parse_replay(\"path/to/game.rep\")\n    for cmd in replay.commands:\n        print(cmd.frame, cmd.msg_type, cmd.player_idx)\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport struct\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any\n\nfrom zh_copilot.replay.message_types import ArgType, ARG_SIZE, MsgType\n\nMAGIC = b\"GENREP\"\nMAX_SLOTS = 8\n\n# Network message range — only these are recorded\nMSG_NET_BEGIN = 1000\nMSG_NET_END   = 1999\nMSG_CLEAR_GAME_DATA = 10  # terminal record\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass SystemTime:\n    \"\"\"Win32 SYSTEMTIME struct (16 bytes = 8 × uint16).\"\"\"\n    year:         int\n    month:        int\n    day_of_week:  int\n    day:          int\n    hour:         int\n    minute:       int\n    second:       int\n    milliseconds: int\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ReplayHeader:\n    \"\"\"Parsed .rep file header.\"\"\"\n    magic:               str\n    start_time:          int          # Unix timestamp\n    end_time:            int          # Unix timestamp\n    frame_duration:      int          # total logic frames\n    desync_game:         bool\n    quit_early:          bool\n    player_discons:      list[bool]   # len=8\n    replay_name:         str\n    time_val:            SystemTime\n    version_string:      str\n    version_time_string: str\n    version_number:      int\n    exe_crc:             int\n    ini_crc:             int\n    game_options:        str          # raw ASCII — parsed by game_options.py\n    local_player_index:  int          # -1 if not applicable\n    difficulty:          int\n    original_game_mode:  int\n    rank_points:         int\n    max_fps:             int\n\n    @property\n    def duration_seconds(self) -> float:\n        return self.frame_duration / 30.0\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ReplayCommand:\n    \"\"\"Single command record from the .rep command stream.\"\"\"\n    frame:      int\n    msg_type:   int                            # raw int (may not be in MsgType enum)\n    player_idx: int\n    args:       dict[ArgType, list[Any]] = field(default_factory=dict)\n\n    @property\n    def is_crc(self) -> bool:\n        return self.msg_type == MSG_NET_BEGIN\n\n    @property\n    def msg_type_name(self) -> str:\n        try:\n            return MsgType(self.msg_type).name\n        except ValueError:\n            return f\"UNKNOWN_{self.msg_type}\"\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ReplayFile:\n    \"\"\"Fully parsed .rep replay file.\"\"\"\n    header:   ReplayHeader\n    commands: list[ReplayCommand]\n\n    @property\n    def gameplay_commands(self) -> list[ReplayCommand]:\n        \"\"\"Commands excluding CRC sync messages.\"\"\"\n        return [c for c in self.commands if not c.is_crc]\n\n    @property\n    def player_commands(self) -> dict[int, list[ReplayCommand]]:\n        \"\"\"Commands grouped by player index.\"\"\"\n        result: dict[int, list[ReplayCommand]] = {}\n        for cmd in self.gameplay_commands:\n            result.setdefault(cmd.player_idx, []).append(cmd)\n        return result\n\n\nclass _BinaryReader:\n    \"\"\"Streaming binary reader over a bytes buffer.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, data: bytes):\n        self._data = data\n        self._pos  = 0\n\n    @property\n    def pos(self) -> int:\n        return self._pos\n\n    @property\n    def remaining(self) -> int:\n        return len(self._data) - self._pos\n\n    def read(self, n: int) -> bytes:\n        if self._pos + n > len(self._data):\n            raise EOFError(f\"Need {n} bytes at offset {self._pos}, only {self.remaining} remain\")\n        chunk = self._data[self._pos:self._pos + n]\n        self._pos += n\n        return chunk\n\n    def unpack(self, fmt: str) -> tuple:\n        size = struct.calcsize(fmt)\n        return struct.unpack(fmt, self.read(size))\n\n    def read_u8(self)  -> int:   return self.unpack(\"<B\")[0]\n    def read_i32(self) -> int:   return self.unpack(\"<i\")[0]\n    def read_u32(self) -> int:   return self.unpack(\"<I\")[0]\n    def read_bool(self) -> bool: return bool(self.unpack(\"<?\")[0])\n    def read_f32(self) -> float: return self.unpack(\"<f\")[0]\n\n    def read_wstr(self) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Read null-terminated UTF-16LE string.\"\"\"\n        chars = []\n        while True:\n            word = self.unpack(\"<H\")[0]\n            if word == 0:\n                break\n            chars.append(word)\n        return \"\".join(chr(c) for c in chars)\n\n    def read_astr(self) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Read null-terminated ASCII string.\"\"\"\n        chars = []\n        while True:\n            byte = self.read(1)[0]\n            if byte == 0:\n                break\n            chars.append(chr(byte))\n        return \"\".join(chars)\n\n\ndef _parse_header(r: _BinaryReader) -> ReplayHeader:\n    # Fixed section (28 bytes)\n    magic_bytes = r.read(6)\n    if magic_bytes != MAGIC:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Invalid magic: expected {MAGIC!r}, got {magic_bytes!r}\")\n\n    start_time     = r.read_u32()\n    end_time       = r.read_u32()\n    frame_duration = r.read_u32()\n    desync_game    = r.read_bool()\n    quit_early     = r.read_bool()\n    player_discons = [r.read_bool() for _ in range(MAX_SLOTS)]\n\n    # Variable section\n    replay_name         = r.read_wstr()\n    time_val_raw        = r.unpack(\"<8H\")\n    time_val            = SystemTime(*time_val_raw)\n    version_string      = r.read_wstr()\n    version_time_string = r.read_wstr()\n    version_number      = r.read_u32()\n    exe_crc             = r.read_u32()\n    ini_crc             = r.read_u32()\n    game_options        = r.read_astr()\n    local_player_str    = r.read_astr()\n    local_player_index  = int(local_player_str) if local_player_str.lstrip(\"-\").isdigit() else -1\n    difficulty          = r.read_i32()\n    original_game_mode  = r.read_i32()\n    rank_points         = r.read_i32()\n    max_fps             = r.read_i32()\n\n    return ReplayHeader(\n        magic               = magic_bytes.decode(\"ascii\"),\n        start_time          = start_time,\n        end_time            = end_time,\n        frame_duration      = frame_duration,\n        desync_game         = desync_game,\n        quit_early          = quit_early,\n        player_discons      = player_discons,\n        replay_name         = replay_name,\n        time_val            = time_val,\n        version_string      = version_string,\n        version_time_string = version_time_string,\n        version_number      = version_number,\n        exe_crc             = exe_crc,\n        ini_crc             = ini_crc,\n        game_options        = game_options,\n        local_player_index  = local_player_index,\n        difficulty          = difficulty,\n        original_game_mode  = original_game_mode,\n        rank_points         = rank_points,\n        max_fps             = max_fps,\n    )\n\n\ndef _parse_arg(r: _BinaryReader, arg_type: ArgType) -> Any:\n    \"\"\"Parse a single argument value of the given type.\"\"\"\n    size = ARG_SIZE[arg_type]\n    if size == 0:\n        return None\n    if arg_type == ArgType.INTEGER:\n        return r.read_i32()\n    if arg_type == ArgType.REAL:\n        return r.read_f32()\n    if arg_type == ArgType.BOOLEAN:\n        return r.read_bool()\n    if arg_type in (ArgType.OBJECTID, ArgType.DRAWABLEID,\n                    ArgType.TEAMID, ArgType.TIMESTAMP):\n        return r.read_u32()\n    if arg_type == ArgType.LOCATION:\n        x, y, z = r.unpack(\"<3f\")\n        return (x, y, z)\n    if arg_type == ArgType.PIXEL:\n        x, y = r.unpack(\"<2i\")\n        return (x, y)\n    if arg_type == ArgType.PIXELREGION:\n        x1, y1, x2, y2 = r.unpack(\"<4i\")\n        return (x1, y1, x2, y2)\n    if arg_type == ArgType.WIDECHAR:\n        return chr(r.unpack(\"<H\")[0])\n    raw = r.read(size)\n    return raw\n\n\ndef _parse_commands(r: _BinaryReader) -> list[ReplayCommand]:\n    commands: list[ReplayCommand] = []\n\n    while r.remaining >= 4:\n        try:\n            frame = r.read_u32()\n        except EOFError:\n            break\n\n        if r.remaining < 9:   # type(4) + player(4) + num_types(1)\n            break\n\n        msg_type   = r.read_u32()\n        player_idx = r.read_i32()\n        num_types  = r.read_u8()\n\n        # Terminal record: MSG_CLEAR_GAME_DATA (10) ends the stream\n        if msg_type == MSG_CLEAR_GAME_DATA and num_types == 0:\n            break\n\n        args: dict[ArgType, list[Any]] = {}\n\n        for _ in range(num_types):\n            if r.remaining < 2:\n                break\n            raw_type  = r.read_u8()\n            arg_count = r.read_u8()\n\n            try:\n                arg_type = ArgType(raw_type)\n            except ValueError:\n                # Unknown arg type — skip if possible\n                break\n\n            values = [_parse_arg(r, arg_type) for _ in range(arg_count)]\n            args[arg_type] = values\n\n        commands.append(ReplayCommand(\n            frame      = frame,\n            msg_type   = msg_type,\n            player_idx = player_idx,\n            args       = args,\n        ))\n\n    return commands\n\n\ndef parse_replay_bytes(data: bytes) -> ReplayFile:\n    \"\"\"Parse a .rep file from raw bytes. Raises ValueError on invalid format.\"\"\"\n    if not data:\n        raise ValueError(\"Empty input\")\n    r = _BinaryReader(data)\n    header   = _parse_header(r)\n    commands = _parse_commands(r)\n    return ReplayFile(header=header, commands=commands)\n\n\ndef parse_replay(path: str | Path) -> ReplayFile:\n    \"\"\"Parse a .rep file from disk.\"\"\"\n    data = Path(path).read_bytes()\n    return parse_replay_bytes(data)\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 5: Run tests**\n\n```bash\npython -m pytest tests/replay/test_parser.py -v\n```\nExpected: all 8 tests PASS\n\n- [ ] Options Sub-Parser\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `src/zh_copilot/replay/game_options.py`\n- Create: `tests/replay/test_game_options.py`\n\nParses the `game_options` ASCII string from the header into structured `GameInfo`.\n\nThe format produced by `GameInfoToAsciiString()` looks like:\n```\nM=maps/official/mp_tournament_desert.map S=1 O=1 C=1 F=America C=2 F=China T=1 T=2 ...\n```\n\nKey tokens:\n- `M=<path>` — map file path\n- `C=<n>` — player slot n\n- `F=<faction>` — faction for most recent slot (`America`, `China`, `GLA`)\n- `T=<n>` — team assignment\n- `H=<n>` — slot is human (1) or AI (0)\n- `S=<n>` — starting cash multiplier\n- `O=<n>` — options flags\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Write failing tests**\n\n```python\n# tests/replay/test_game_options.py\n\"\"\"Tests for the game_options string sub-parser.\"\"\"\n\nimport pytest\nfrom zh_copilot.replay.game_options import parse_game_options, GameInfo, PlayerSlot\n\n\ndef test_minimal_1v1():\n    opts = \"M=maps/alpine.map C=1 F=America H=1 T=1 C=2 F=China H=0 T=2\"\n    info = parse_game_options(opts)\n    assert info.map_path == \"maps/alpine.map\"\n    assert len(info.slots) == 2\n    assert info.slots[0].faction == \"America\"\n    assert info.slots[0].is_human is True\n    assert info.slots[0].team == 1\n    assert info.slots[1].faction == \"China\"\n    assert info.slots[1].is_human is False\n\n\ndef test_faction_gla():\n    opts = \"M=maps/test.map C=1 F=GLA H=1 T=1\"\n    info = parse_game_options(opts)\n    assert info.slots[0].faction == \"GLA\"\n\n\ndef test_map_path_extracted():\n    opts = \"M=maps/official/mp_tournament_desert.map C=1 F=America H=1 T=1\"\n    info = parse_game_options(opts)\n    assert info.map_path == \"maps/official/mp_tournament_desert.map\"\n\n\ndef test_num_players():\n    opts = \"M=maps/x.map C=1 F=America H=1 T=1 C=2 F=China H=1 T=2\"\n    info = parse_game_options(opts)\n    assert info.num_players == 2\n\n\ndef test_faction_index():\n    opts = \"M=maps/x.map C=1 F=America H=1 T=1\"\n    info = parse_game_options(opts)\n    assert info.slots[0].faction_index == 0   # USA=0\n\n    opts2 = \"M=maps/x.map C=1 F=GLA H=1 T=1\"\n    info2 = **Step 3: Implement game_options.py**\n\n```python\n# src/zh_copilot/replay/game_options.py\n\"\"\"Parser for the gameOptions ASCII string from .rep file headers.\n\nThe gameOptions string is produced by GameInfoToAsciiString() in the engine.\nIt encodes map, players, factions, teams in a space-separated token format.\n\nExample:\n  \"M=maps/mp_tournament.map S=1 C=1 F=America H=1 T=1 C=2 F=China H=0 T=2\"\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\n\nFACTION_INDEX = {\"America\": 0, \"China\": 1, \"GLA\": 2}\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass PlayerSlot:\n    \"\"\"One player slot parsed from game_options.\"\"\"\n    slot_number: int          # 1-based slot index (from C=N token)\n    faction:     str          # \"America\", \"China\", \"GLA\", or \"Random\"\n    is_human:    bool         # True if H=1\n    team:        int          # team number from T=N\n\n    @property\n    def faction_index(self) -> int:\n        \"\"\"0=USA, 1=China, 2=GLA, -1=unknown.\"\"\"\n        return FACTION_INDEX.get(self.faction, -1)\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass GameInfo:\n    \"\"\"Structured game information from the game_options header string.\"\"\"\n    map_path:   str               = \"\"\n    slots:      list[PlayerSlot]  = field(default_factory=list)\n    raw:        str               = \"\"\n\n    @property\n    def num_players(self) -> int:\n        return len(self.slots)\n\n    @property\n    def factions(self) -> list[str]:\n        return [s.faction for s in self.slots]\n\n    @property\n    def is_1v1(self) -> bool:\n        return self.num_players == 2\n\n\ndef parse_game_options(options_str: str) -> GameInfo:\n    \"\"\"Parse game_options ASCII string into GameInfo.\n\n    Args:\n        options_str: Raw ASCII string from ReplayHeader.game_options\n\n    Returns:\n        GameInfo with map path and player slots populated.\n    \"\"\"\n    info = GameInfo(raw=options_str)\n    if not options_str:\n        return info\n\n    tokens = options_str.split()\n    current_slot: PlayerSlot | None = None\n\n    for token in tokens:\n        if \"=\" not in token:\n            continue\n        key, _, value = token.partition(\"=\")\n\n        if key == \"M\":\n            info.map_path = value\n        elif key == \"C\":\n            # New player slot\n            if current_slot is not None:\n                info.slots.append(current_slot)\n            try:\n                slot_num = int(value)\n            except ValueError:\n                slot_num = len(info.slots) + 1\n            current_slot = PlayerSlot(\n                slot_number=slot_num,\n                faction=\"Unknown\",\n                is_human=False,\n                team=0,\n            )\n        elif key == \"F\" and current_slot is not None:\n            current_slot.faction = value\n        elif key == \"H\" and current_slot is not None:\n            current_slot.is_human = (value == \"1\")\n        elif key == \"T\" and current_slot is not None:\n            try:\n                current_slot.team = int(value)\n            except ValueError:\n                pass\n\n    # Append last slot\n    if current_slot is not None:\n        info.slots.append(current_slot)\n\n    return info\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: Run tests**\n\n```bash\npython -m pytest tests/replay/test_game_options.py -v\n```\nExpected: all 6 tests PASS\n\n- [ ] **Step 5: Commit**\n\n```bash\ngit add src/zh_copilot/replay/game_options.py tests/replay/test_game_options.py\ngit commit -m \"feat(replay): game_options string parser — map, factions, teams\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### Task 4: Replay Analysis Tools\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `src/zh_copilot/replay/analysis.py`\n- Create: `tests/replay/test_analysis.py`\n\nTools for filtering and quality-checking replays before using them for training.\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Write failing tests**\n\n```python\n# tests/replay/test_analysis.py\n\"\"\"Tests for replay analysis and quality filtering.\"\"\"\n\nimport io, struct\nimport pytest\nfrom zh_copilot.replay.analysis import (\n    ReplayStats,\n    compute_stats,\n    is_valid_for_training,\n    filter_replays,\n)\nfrom zh_copilot.replay.parser import ReplayFile, ReplayHeader, ReplayCommand, SystemTime\nfrom zh_copilot.replay.message_types import MsgType, ArgType\n\n\ndef make_header(frame_duration=9000, desync=False, quit_early=False) -> ReplayHeader:\n    return ReplayHeader(\n        magic=\"GENREP\", start_time=0, end_time=300,\n        frame_duration=frame_duration, desync_game=desync, quit_early=quit_early,\n        player_discons=[False]*8, replay_name=\"test\",\n        time_val=SystemTime(2024,1,1,1,0,0,0,0),\n        version_string=\"1.04\", version_time_string=\"\", version_number=10401,\n        exe_crc=0, ini_crc=0,\n        game_options=\"M=maps/alpine.map C=1 F=America H=1 T=1 C=2 F=China H=1 T=2\",\n        local_player_index=0, difficulty=1, original_game_mode=7,\n        rank_points=0, max_fps=30,\n    )\n\n\ndef make_command(frame: int, msg_type: int, player_idx: int) -> ReplayCommand:\n    return ReplayCommand(frame=frame, msg_type=msg_type, player_idx=player_idx)\n\n\ndef test_compute_stats_duration():\n    header = make_header(frame_duration=9000)\n    commands = [make_command(f*300, MsgType.DO_MOVETO, 0) for f in range(30)]\n    replay = ReplayFile(header=header, commands=commands)\n    stats = compute_stats(replay)\n    assert stats.duration_seconds == pytest.approx(300.0)\n\n\ndef test_compute_stats_command_counts():\n    header = make_header()\n    commands = [\n        make_command(100, MsgType.DO_MOVETO, 0),\n        make_command(200, MsgType.DO_MOVETO, 1),\n        make_command(300, MsgType.DOZER_CONSTRUCT, 0),\n    ]\n    replay = ReplayFile(header=header, commands=commands)\n    stats = compute_stats(replay)\n    assert stats.total_commands == 3\n    assert stats.commands_per_player[0] == 2\n    assert stats.commands_per_player[1] == 1\n\n\ndef test_valid_for_training_normal():\n    header = make_header(frame_duration=9000)\n    commands = [make_command(f*30, MsgType.DO_MOVETO, f%2) for f in range(100)]\n    replay = ReplayFile(header=header, commands=commands)\n    assert is_valid_for_training(replay) is True\n\n\ndef test_invalid_desync():\n    header = make_header(desync=True)\n    replay = ReplayFile(header=header, commands=[])\n    assert is_valid_for_training(replay) is False\n\n\ndef test_invalid_too_short():\n    header = make_header(frame_duration=600)  # 20 seconds — too short\n    replay = ReplayFile(header=header, commands=[])\n    assert is_valid_for_training(replay) is False\n\n\ndef test_filter_replays():\n    good = ReplayFile(header=make_header(9000), commands=[\n        make_command(f*30, MsgType.DO_MOVETO, f%2) for f in range(50)\n    ])\n    bad = ReplayFile(header=make_header(desync=True), commands=[])\n    result = filter_replays([good, bad])\n    assert len(result) == 1\n    assert result[0] is good\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Run tests to verify they fail**\n\n```bash\npython -m pytest tests/replay/test_analysis.py -v\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: Implement analysis.py**\n\n```python\n# src/zh_copilot/replay/analysis.py\n\"\"\"Replay analysis and quality filtering tools.\n\nUsed to filter collected replays before using them for training.\nRejects: desynced games, very short games, games with disconnections.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\n\nfrom zh_copilot.replay.parser import ReplayFile, ReplayCommand\nfrom zh_copilot.replay.game_options import parse_game_options\n\n# Training quality thresholds\nMIN_DURATION_SECONDS = 60.0    # reject games shorter than 1 minute\nMIN_COMMANDS         = 20      # reject games with very few commands\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ReplayStats:\n    \"\"\"Summary statistics for a parsed replay.\"\"\"\n    duration_seconds:    float\n    total_commands:      int\n    commands_per_player: dict[int, int] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    num_players:         int = 0\n    map_path:            str = \"\"\n    factions:            list[str] = field(default_factory=list)\n    version_number:      int = 0\n    desync_game:         bool = False\n    quit_early:          bool = False\n\n\ndef compute_stats(replay: ReplayFile) -> ReplayStats:\n    \"\"\"Compute summary statistics from a parsed replay.\"\"\"\n    gameplay_cmds = replay.gameplay_commands\n    per_player: dict[int, int] = {}\n    for cmd in gameplay_cmds:\n        per_player[cmd.player_idx] = per_player.get(cmd.player_idx, 0) + 1\n\n    game_info = parse_game_options(replay.header.game_options)\n\n    return ReplayStats(\n        duration_seconds    = replay.header.duration_seconds,\n        total_commands      = len(gameplay_cmds),\n        commands_per_player = per_player,\n        num_players         = game_info.num_players,\n        map_path            = game_info.map_path,\n        factions            = game_info.factions,\n        version_number      = replay.header.version_number,\n        desync_game         = replay.header.desync_game,\n        quit_early          = replay.header.quit_early,\n    )\n\n\ndef is_valid_for_training(\n    replay: ReplayFile,\n    min_duration: float = MIN_DURATION_SECONDS,\n    min_commands: int   = MIN_COMMANDS,\n) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Return True if the replay meets quality thresholds for training.\n\n    Rejects:\n    - Desynced games (CRC mismatch — game state diverged from expected)\n    - Very short games (likely forfeit or error)\n    - Games with too few commands (AI idle bug or load failure)\n    - Games with player disconnections\n    \"\"\"\n    if replay.header.desync_game:\n        return False\n    if replay.header.duration_seconds < min_duration:\n        return False\n    if any(replay.header.player_discons):\n        return False\n    gameplay_cmds = replay.gameplay_commands\n    if len(gameplay_cmds) < min_commands:\n        return False\n    return True\n\n\ndef filter_replays(\n    replays: list[ReplayFile],\n    min_duration: float = MIN_DURATION_SECONDS,\n    min_commands: int   = MIN_COMMANDS,\n) -> list[ReplayFile]:\n    \"\"\"Filter a list of replays, returning only those valid for training.\"\"\"\n    return [\n        r for r in replays\n        if is_valid_for_training(r, min_duration, min_commands)\n    ]\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: Run tests**\n\n```bash\npython -m pytest tests/replay/test_analysis.py -v\n```\nExpected: all 7 tests PASS\n\n- [ ] **Step 5: Commit**\n\n```bash\ngit add src/zh_copilot/replay/runner.py\n\"\"\"Replay runner — plays .rep files through the headless engine.\n\nConnects to the TrainingServer (Plan 1) and steps through each\ncommand in the replay, recording game state at each decision step.\n\nREQUIRES: zh_headless.exe running on Windows VM.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport numpy as np\n\nfrom zh_copilot.replay.parser import ReplayFile, parse_replay, ReplayCommand\nfrom zh_copilot.replay.message_types import MsgType, ArgType\nfrom zh_copilot.engine.client import EngineClient\nfrom zh_copilot.state.game_state import Action\n\n\nclass ReplayRunner:\n    \"\"\"Steps a replay through the headless engine and records observations.\n\n    At each decision step (every 10 ticks), records:\n      - GameStateObservation (state before the decision)\n      - ActionCommand (the command issued at this step, if any)\n      - Outcome (win/loss, extracted from final state)\n\n    Args:\n        engine_host: IP of the Windows VM running zh_headless.exe\n        engine_port: ZeroMQ port (default 5555)\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, engine_host: str = \"localhost\", engine_port: int = 5555):\n        self._client = EngineClient(host=engine_host, port=engine_port)\n\n    def run(self, replay: ReplayFile) -> list[dict]:\n        \"\"\"Run a replay and return list of (state, action, frame) dicts.\n\n        Returns:\n            List of step dicts:\n              {\n                \"frame\": int,\n                \"state\": GameStateObservation bytes,\n                \"action_type\": int,\n                \"player_idx\": int,\n              }\n        \"\"\"\n        self._client.connect()\n        steps = []\n\n        # Index commands by frame for fast lookup\n        cmd_by_frame: dict[int, list[ReplayCommand]] = {}\n        for cmd in replay.gameplay_commands:\n            cmd_by_frame.setdefault(cmd.frame, []).append(cmd)\n\n        obs = self._client.reset()\n        total_frames = replay.header.frame_duration\n        decision_ticks = 10  # matches TrainingServer.DECISION_TICKS\n\n        frame = 0\n        while frame < total_frames:\n            # Find commands for this decision window\n            window_cmds = []\n            for f in range(frame, frame + decision_ticks):\n                window_cmds.extend(cmd_by_frame.get(f, []))\n\n            # Use first command in window as the action label\n            action = Action.noop()\n            action_player = 0\n            if window_cmds:\n                cmd = window_cmds[0]\n                action = Action(action_type=self._map_msg_to_action(cmd.msg_type))\n                action_player = cmd.player_idx\n\n            obs, _, done, _ = self._client.step(action, player_idx=action_player)\n\n            steps.append({\n                \"frame\":       frame,\n                \"action_type\": action.action_type,\n                \"player_idx\":  action_player,\n                \"game_time\":   obs.global_state.game_time,\n            })\n\n            frame += decision_ticks\n            if done:\n                break\n\n        self._client.close()\n        return steps\n\n    def _map_msg_to_action(self, msg_type: int) -> int:\n        \"\"\"Map GameMessage type to ActionType enum index.\"\"\"\n        from zh_copilot.replay.message_types import MsgType\n        from zh_copilot.engine.client import ACT_MOVE, ACT_ATTACK_MOVE, ACT_ATTACK_TARGET\n        mapping = {\n            MsgType.DO_MOVETO:                 4,   # ACT_MOVE\n            MsgType.DO_ATTACKMOVETO:           5,   # ACT_ATTACK_MOVE\n            MsgType.DO_ATTACK_OBJECT:          9,   # ACT_ATTACK_TARGET\n            MsgType.DOZER_CONSTRUCT:           0,   # ACT_BUILD_STRUCTURE\n            MsgType.QUEUE_UNIT_CREATE:         1,   # ACT_TRAIN_UNIT\n            MsgType.SELL:                      2,   # ACT_SELL_BUILDING\n            MsgType.SET_RALLY_POINT:           3,   # ACT_SET_RALLY_POINT\n            MsgType.QUEUE_UPGRADE:             13,  # ACT_RESEARCH_UPGRADE\n            MsgType.PURCHASE_SCIENCE:          14,  # ACT_CHOOSE_GENERAL_POWER\n            MsgType.DO_SPECIAL_POWER_AT_LOCATION: 12,  # ACT_USE_ABILITY\n        }\n        return mapping.get(msg_type, 20)  # default: noop\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Commit**\n\n```bash\ngit add src/zh_copilot/replay/runner.py\ngit commit -m \"feat(replay): ReplayRunner — steps replays through headless engine\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### Task 7: HDF5 Dataset Writer (Intel Mac / Windows — requires Plan 1)\n\n> **Requires:** Plan 1 headless engine.\n> Skip until engine available.\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `src/zh_copilot/replay/dataset.py`\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Install h5py**\n\n```bash\npip install h5py --quiet\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Implement dataset.py**\n\n```python\n# src/zh_copilot/replay/dataset.py\n\"\"\"HDF5 dataset writer and reader for replay training data.\n\nWrites (state, action, outcome) tuples from ReplayRunner to HDF5.\nRead back by the imitation learning training pipeline.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Iterator\n\nimport h5py\nimport numpy as np\n\nfrom zh_copilot.config import STATE\n\n\nclass ReplayDatasetWriter:\n    \"\"\"Writes replay step data to HDF5 file.\n\n    HDF5 layout:\n        /spatial      (N, 40, 128, 128) float32\n        /entity_features (N, 1024, 32) float32\n        /entity_mask  (N, 1024) bool\n        /global_scalars (N, 80) float32\n        /action_type  (N,) int32\n        /player_idx   (N,) int32\n        /frame        (N,) int32\n        /game_time    (N,) float32\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, path: str | Path, chunk_size: int = 256):\n        self._path = Path(path)\n        self._chunk = chunk_size\n        self._file: h5py.File | None = None\n        self._n = 0\n\n    def open(self) -> None:\n        self._file = h5py.File(self._path, \"w\")\n        R = STATE.spatial_resolution\n        C = STATE.spatial_channels\n        N = STATE.max_entities\n        D = STATE.entity_feature_dim\n        S = STATE.global_scalar_dim\n\n        kw = dict(maxshape=(None,), chunks=(self._chunk,))\n        self._file.create_dataset(\"action_type\",  shape=(0,), dtype=\"i4\", **kw)\n        self._file.create_dataset(\"player_idx\",   shape=(0,), dtype=\"i4\", **kw)\n        self._file.create_dataset(\"frame\",        shape=(0,), dtype=\"i4\", **kw)\n        self._file.create_dataset(\"game_time\",    shape=(0,), dtype=\"f4\", **kw)\n\n        kw2d = dict(maxshape=(None, C, R, R), chunks=(self._chunk, C, R, R))\n        self._file.create_dataset(\"spatial\", shape=(0, C, R, R), dtype=\"f4\", **kw2d)\n\n        self._file.create_dataset(\"entity_features\",\n                                   shape=(0, N, D), dtype=\"f4\",\n                                   maxshape=(None, N, D),\n                                   chunks=(self._chunk, N, D))\n        self._file.create_dataset(\"entity_mask\",\n                                   shape=(0, N), dtype=bool,\n                                   maxshape=(None, N),\n                                   chunks=(self._chunk, N))\n        self._file.create_dataset(\"global_scalars\",\n                                   shape=(0, S), dtype=\"f4\",\n                                   maxshape=(None, S),\n                                   chunks=(self._chunk, S))\n\n    def write_step(self, step: dict) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Write a single step dict to the dataset.\"\"\"\n        if self._file is None:\n            raise RuntimeError(\"Call open() first\")\n        n = self._n\n        for ds in self._file.values():\n            ds.resize(n + 1, axis=0)\n\n        self._file[\"action_type\"][n] = step.get(\"action_type\", 20)\n        self._file[\"player_idx\"][n]  = step.get(\"player_idx\", 0)\n        self._file[\"frame\"][n]       = step.get(\"frame\", 0)\n        self._file[\"game_time\"][n]   = step.get(\"game_time\", 0.0)\n        # spatial/entity/scalars: write zeros if not provided (engine not available)\n        self._file[\"spatial\"][n]         = step.get(\"spatial\", np.zeros((STATE.spatial_channels, STATE.spatial_resolution, STATE.spatial_resolution), dtype=np.float32))\n        self._file[\"entity_features\"][n] = step.get(\"entity_features\", np.zeros((STATE.max_entities, STATE.entity_feature_dim), dtype=np.float32))\n        self._file[\"entity_mask\"][n]     = step.get(\"entity_mask\", np.zeros(STATE.max_entities, dtype=bool))\n        self._file[\"global_scalars\"][n]  = step.get(\"global_scalars\", np.zeros(STATE.global_scalar_dim, dtype=np.float32))\n        self._n += 1\n\n    def close(self) -> None:\n        if self._file:\n            self._file.attrs[\"num_steps\"] = self._n\n            self._file.close()\n            self._file = None\n\n    def __enter__(self) -> ReplayDatasetWriter:\n        self.open()\n        return self\n\n    def __exit__(self, *args) -> None:\n        self.close()\n\n\nclass ReplayDataset:\n    \"\"\"Read replay HDF5 datasets for training.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, path: str | Path):\n        self._path = Path(path)\n        self._file = h5py.File(self._path, \"r\")\n\n    def __len__(self) -> int:\n        return int(self._file.attrs.get(\"num_steps\", 0))\n\n    def __getitem__(self, idx: int) -> dict:\n        return {\n            \"action_type\":    int(self._file[\"action_type\"][idx]),\n            \"player_idx\":     int(self._file[\"player_idx\"][idx]),\n            \"frame\":          int(self._file[\"frame\"][idx]),\n            \"spatial\":        self._file[\"spatial\"][idx],\n            \"entity_features\": self._file[\"entity_features\"][idx],\n            \"entity_mask\":    self._file[\"entity_mask\"][idx],\n            \"global_scalars\": self._file[\"global_scalars\"][idx],\n        }\n\n    def close(self) -> None:\n        self._file.close()\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: Commit**\n\n```bash\ngit add src/zh_copilot/replay/dataset.py\ngit commit -m \"feat(replay): HDF5 dataset writer and reader for training pipeline\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## Plan 2 Complete Checklist\n\n**M4 Mac (can do now):**\n- [ ] `python -m pytest tests/replay/ -v` — all tests pass\n- [ ] `python -m pytest tests/ -v` — full suite passes\n- [ ] `parse_replay_bytes(make_minimal_replay())` returns valid `ReplayFile`\n- [ ] `parse_game_options(\"M=maps/x.map C=1 F=America H=1 T=1\")` returns correct The plan has been saved to `/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/zh-copilot/plans/plan-7-copilot-ui.md`.

Here is a summary of what was written:

**Structure:** 9 tasks, 22 numbered steps, all with checkbox syntax for agentic tracking.

**Task breakdown:**

1. **UIConfig** (Step 1) — adds a `UIConfig` dataclass and `UI` singleton to `config.py` covering geometry, colours, fonts, poll interval, and hotkey strings.

2. **StrategyState** (Steps 2–7) — 18 tests covering defaults, read/write validation (raises `ValueError` on out-of-range inputs), atomic `snapshot()`, 100-concurrent-writer stress test, and a reader/writer interleaving test. Implementation uses a single `threading.Lock` wrapping all five fields.

3. **HotkeyListener** (Steps 8–11) — 12 tests that bypass pynput by calling `_build_hotkey_mapping()` on a `__new__`-constructed instance, verifying the 8 registered keys (F1–F6, F9, F10), their callbacks, and the default-arg closure correctness. Two init tests monkeypatch `sys.modules` to stub pynput entirely.

4. **Overlay** (Steps 12–15) — 12 tests covering widget creation (6 buttons, status label, win canvas), state sync (button highlight on `_refresh()`, status text including "PAUSED" branch, win bar canvas items), layout (frame width matches config, F-key hints in button text), and button click → `StrategyState` write-through.

5. **UIRunner** (Step 16) — wires all three components; the bot loop runs in a daemon thread, pynput in its own daemon, tkinter on the main thread. Clean shutdown on F10, window close, or SIGINT.

6. **Entry point** (Steps 17–18) — `scripts/run_copilot.py` with argparse (`--checkpoint`, `--mode`, `--engine-host`, `--engine-port`, `--device`) and a startup banner with the hotkey table.

7. **Package exports** (Step 19) — updates `deploy/__init__.py`.

8. **Smoke test** (Step 20) — `test_ui_runner.py` stubs pynput and `CopilotRunner`, calls `runner.run()` in a thread, asserts it returns within 5 seconds and `state.running` is False.

9. **Dependency** (Step 22) — adds `pynput>=1.7` to `pyproject.toml`.

1	# Plan 3: AI Model Architecture
2	
3	> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.
4	
5	**Goal:** Implement the full ZH-Copilot neural network in PyTorch — Custom CNN (spatial), Transformer (entities), MLP (scalars), LSTM (temporal), Strategy Encoder, Action Decoder, and Value Head. Model runs on M4 Mac via MPS backend.
6	
7	**Architecture:** Five encoders fuse into an LSTM. The LSTM output feeds three heads: policy (action decoder), value (win probability), and strategy (game phase embedding). The action decoder is auto-regressive: action type → target pointer → spatial position. All hyperparameters come from `config.MODEL`.
8	
9	**Tech Stack:** PyTorch 2.x, MPS (Apple Silicon GPU), pytest
10	
11	---
12	
13	## File Structure
14	
15	```
16	src/zh_copilot/model/
17	├── __init__.py              # exports ZHCopilotModel + forward signature
18	├── spatial_encoder.py       # Custom CNN: (B, C, H, W) → (B, cnn_output_dim)
19	├── entity_encoder.py        # Transformer: (B, N, D) → (B, N, dim) + pooled (B, dim)
20	├── scalar_encoder.py        # MLP: (B, scalar_dim) → (B, mlp_hidden)
21	├── temporal.py              # LSTM: fused → (B, lstm_hidden) + hidden state
22	├── strategy_encoder.py      # Strategy: lstm_out → (B, strategy_dim)
23	├── action_decoder.py        # Action type + pointer + spatial heads
24	├── value_head.py            # Win probability: (B, lstm_hidden) → (B, 1)
25	└── zh_model.py              # ZHCopilotModel: wires everything together
26	
27	tests/model/
28	├── __init__.py
29	├── test_spatial_encoder.py
30	├── test_entity_encoder.py
31	├── test_scalar_encoder.py
32	├── test_temporal.py
33	├── test_action_decoder.py
34	├── test_value_head.py
35	└── test_zh_model.py
36	```
37	
38	---
39	
40	### Task 1: Spatial Encoder (Custom CNN)
41	
42	**Files:**
43	- Create: `src/zh_copilot/model/spatial_encoder.py`
44	- Create: `tests/model/test_spatial_encoder.py`
45	- Create: `tests/model/__init__.py`
46	
47	Input: `(B, 40, 128, 128)` spatial map tensor
48	Output: `(B, 256)` feature vector
49	
50	Architecture: 6 residual conv blocks with squeeze-excitation attention.
51	Progressive downsampling: 128→64→32→16→8→4. Final global average pool → linear.
